1. **初始画面的a b z默认项应该是z**：当前默认选中的是a，需要改成默认选中z。
2. **当滑块移动时，无论自动还是手动，下面2D面板相应参数要自动跟随变化**：需要确保2D面板的公式提示和曲线能实时反映当前参数变化。
3. **3D surface and section右边的-rotate/zoom，改成当前的表达式，以及相应的参数数值**：需要把面板标题旁边的提示文字从"— Rotate/Zoom"改成显示当前表达式和参数值。

   JavaScript 复制
   
   ```js
   function updateZ(val) {
     zVal = Math.max(-20, Math.min(20, parseFloat(val) || 0));
   ```

   这里确实没有在activeParam不是"z"时更新formula。当自动播放z变化时，如果activeParam是a或b，formula不会更新。需要在这里加上`updateFormula()`。另外，用户说"下面2D面板相应参数要自动跟随变化"，可能还指2D面板的公式提示要始终显示当前值。当前`updateFormula()`只在特定时候调用，需要确保所有参数变化时都调用。
3. 3D面板标题：当前是`<span class="hint">— Rotate/Zoom</span>`，需要改成显示当前表达式`z = ax³ + bx - y²`以及a, b, z的当前数值。

JavaScript 复制

```js
function updateSliderConfig() {
  if (activeParam === "a") {
```

     sin θ * x + cos θ * (ax³ + bx - y²) = zVal

   - y² = ax³ + bx + (sin θ * x - zVal) / cos θ

   1. 当 |cos θ| > 0.01 时，使用公式 y² = ax³ + bx + (sin θ * x - zVal) / cos θ，以 x 为横轴，y 为纵轴
   2. 当 |cos θ| ≤ 0.01 时，切换到显示 y 与 z 的关系（因为此时 x 近似为常数）
   
   但这会让代码变得复杂。让我重新考虑用户的意图。也许用户添加 θ 只是为了在 3D 中旋转截面，而 2D 面板仍然显示水平截面的曲线？不，用户说"2D面板相应参数要自动跟随变化"，这意味着 θ 变化时 2D 也应该更新。我觉得我应该实现一个合理的 2D 显示。让我这样做：对于一般 θ，截面方程：sin θ * x + cos θ * z = zVal
   结合 z = ax³ + bx - y²：如果 |cos θ| > 1e-3：
   y² = ax³ + bx - (zVal - sin θ * x) / cos θ
   在 2D 中以 x 为横轴，y 为纵轴绘制如果 |cos θ| ≤ 1e-3（即 θ ≈ 90°）：
   x ≈ zVal / sin θ = zVal（假设 sin θ ≈ 1）
   交线为 z = a*zVal³ + b*zVal - y²
   在 2D 中以 y 为横轴，z 为纵轴绘制但这需要动态改变坐标轴标签和范围，比较复杂。另一种更简单的方法：2D 面板始终显示截面曲线在 **xy 平面上的投影**。也就是说，不管截面如何旋转，2D 面板显示的是 3D 交线投影到 xy 平面上的形状。对于旋转截面 sin θ * x + cos θ * z = zVal，交线满足：
   z = ax³ + bx - y²
   sin θ * x + cos θ * (ax³ + bx - y²) = zVal投影到 xy 平面就是上述方程（消去 z）。这正是 y² = ax³ + bx + (sin θ * x - zVal) / cos θ（当 cos θ ≠ 0）。当 cos θ = 0 时，投影到 xy 平面是直线 x = zVal（因为 x 固定，y 和 z 变化，投影到 xy 平面就是 x = zVal 的垂直线）。所以 2D 面板可以始终显示这个投影：
   
   - 当 cos θ ≠ 0：显示 y² = ax³ + bx + (sin θ * x - zVal) / cos θ 的曲线
   - 当 cos θ = 0：显示 x = zVal 的垂直线（以及可能的提示）
   
   这看起来是合理的。2D 面板显示的是截面曲线在 xy 平面上的投影。但等等，当 θ = 90° 时，截面是 x = zVal，交线是抛物线 z = a*zVal³ + b*zVal - y²。这个抛物线在 xy 平面上的投影是 x = zVal（一条垂直线），y 从 -∞ 到 +∞。这失去了曲线的形状信息。所以投影方法在 θ = 90° 时不好。让我再想想... 也许 2D 面板应该显示截面曲线在截面自身平面上的"展开图"？如果截面绕 y 轴旋转 θ 角，截面平面上的坐标可以定义为：

   让我定义截面局部坐标：
   
   - 截面法向量 n = (sin θ, 0, cos θ)
   - 截面内的一个方向 e1 = (cos θ, 0, -sin θ)（沿 xz 平面，垂直于 n）
   - 截面内的另一个方向 e2 = (0, 1, 0)（y 轴方向，垂直于 n 和 e1）
   
   对于交线上的点 (x, y, z)，它在截面局部坐标系中的坐标为：
   
   - u = x * cos θ - z * sin θ（沿 e1 方向）
   - v = y（沿 e2 方向）

   u = x * cos θ - (ax³ + bx - y²) * sin θ这给出了参数方程，但不容易消去 x 得到 u 和 v 的直接关系。这太复杂了。我觉得对于 2D 面板，最简单的方法是：

   **2. 删除 Record 和 Export，添加 θ 控制**在 toolbar 的第三行（toolbar-row），当前有 Play、Down、Record、Export 按钮。我需要：

   **3. 修改截面计算**当前截面是水平平面 z = zVal。改为旋转平面：
   
   - 绕 y 轴旋转 θ 角
   - 平面方程：sin(θ_rad) * x + cos(θ_rad) * z = zVal
   
   其中 θ_rad = θ * π / 180。交线计算：
   将 z = ax³ + bx - y² 代入平面方程：
   sin(θ) * x + cos(θ) * (ax³ + bx - y²) = zVal解出 y²：
   cos(θ) * y² = cos(θ) * (ax³ + bx) + sin(θ) * x - zVal当 cos(θ) ≠ 0：
   y² = ax³ + bx + (sin(θ) * x - zVal) / cos(θ)当 cos(θ) = 0（θ = 90°）：
   sin(θ) * x = zVal
   x = zVal / sin(θ) = zVal（因为 sin(90°) = 1）此时交线是 x = zVal 平面上的抛物线：z = a*zVal³ + b*zVal - y²对于 3D 可视化：
   
   - cutPlane 需要绕 y 轴旋转 θ 角
   - 交线需要在 3D 中正确计算
   
   对于 2D 可视化：
   
   - 当 cos(θ) ≠ 0：绘制 y² = ax³ + bx + (sin(θ) * x - zVal) / cos(θ)
   - 当 cos(θ) ≈ 0：绘制 x = zVal 的垂直线（或特殊处理）
   
   让我重新考虑 2D 可视化。用户可能期望 2D 面板显示截面曲线的"真实形状"，而不是投影。如果截面绕 y 轴旋转 θ 角，截面平面内的坐标可以定义为：
   
   - 沿截面与 xz 平面交线方向：u = x * cos θ + z * sin θ
   - 沿 y 轴方向：v = y
   
   但这不太对。让我正确定义：截面法向量 n = (sin θ, 0, cos θ)
   截面内两个正交方向：
   
   - e_u = (cos θ, 0, -sin θ)（在 xz 平面内，垂直于 n）
   - e_v = (0, 1, 0)（y 轴方向）
   
   对于交线上的点 P = (x, y, z)，其在截面局部坐标系中的坐标为：
   
   - u = P · e_u = x * cos θ - z * sin θ
   - v = P · e_v = y
   
   代入 z = ax³ + bx - y² = ax³ + bx - v²：
   u = x * cos θ - (ax³ + bx - v²) * sin θ这给出了参数方程，但不容易消去 x。对于 2D 面板，如果我们固定 θ 并绘制 u-v 曲线，需要解出 x 关于 u 和 v 的关系，这通常需要数值方法。这太复杂了。我觉得对于 2D 面板，显示投影到 xy 平面的曲线是更实际的选择。但让我再想想... 当 θ = 0° 时，u = x，v = y，2D 面板显示的就是 x-y 曲线。当 θ = 90° 时，u = -z，v = y，2D 面板应该显示 u-v 曲线，即 z-y 曲线。如果我们始终让 2D 面板的横轴表示"沿截面方向的 x 分量"，纵轴表示 y，那当 θ 变化时，坐标轴的含义也在变化。为了简单起见，我决定：
   
   - 2D 面板始终显示 y 关于某个横轴变量的关系
   - 当 θ 不接近 90° 时，横轴是 x，曲线是 y² = ax³ + bx + (sin θ * x - zVal) / cos θ
   - 当 θ 接近 90° 时，显示提示并简化绘制
   
   实际上，我觉得用户可能并不在意 2D 面板在 θ 接近 90° 时的精确行为。只要大部分情况下（θ 远离 90°）显示正确即可。让我采用这个方案，并在 θ = 90° 时显示一个特殊提示。**4. 修改 3D 面板标题**上一轮修改中，3D 面板标题变成了：
   `— z = -1.00x³ + 1.00x - y² (a=-1.00, b=1.00, z=0.00)`用户现在要求去掉括号部分，只保留表达式：

   让我详细考虑 getIntervals 的修改。当前 getIntervals 计算的是 y² = ax³ + bx - zVal > 0 的区间。对于旋转截面，当 cos θ ≠ 0：
   y² = ax³ + bx + (sin θ * x - zVal) / cos θ
   = ax³ + bx + tan θ * x - zVal / cos θ
   = ax³ + (b + tan θ) * x - zVal / cos θ等等，这不对。让我重新推导：sin θ * x + cos θ * z = zVal
   z = (zVal - sin θ * x) / cos θ = zVal/cos θ - tan θ * x代入 z = ax³ + bx - y²：
   zVal/cos θ - tan θ * x = ax³ + bx - y²
   y² = ax³ + bx + tan θ * x - zVal/cos θ
   y² = ax³ + (b + tan θ) * x - zVal/cos θ当 θ = 0°：tan θ = 0，cos θ = 1，y² = ax³ + bx - zVal ✓当 θ = 90°：cos θ = 0，公式失效。所以 getIntervals 可以修改为：
   
   JavaScript 复制
   
   ```js
   function f(x) {
     if (Math.abs(Math.cos(thetaRad)) < 1e-10) {
       // θ ≈ 90°，x 必须为 zVal / sin θ
       return x === zVal / Math.sin(thetaRad) ? 0 : -1;
     }
     return a * x * x * x + (b + Math.tan(thetaRad)) * x - zVal / Math.cos(thetaRad);
   }
   ```
   
   不，这样不对。当 θ = 90° 时，x 被固定为 zVal，y 可以取任意值使得 z = ax³ + bx - y²。此时对于固定的 x，y² = ax³ + bx - z，其中 z 由平面方程决定。但在 x = zVal 处，z = a*zVal³ + b*zVal - y²，而平面方程要求 sin(90°)*zVal + cos(90°)*z = zVal，即 zVal = zVal，这对任意 z 都成立。所以交线是 x = zVal 平面上的整条抛物线 z = a*zVal³ + b*zVal - y²。对于 2D 面板，当 θ = 90° 时，我们可以显示这条抛物线在 yz 平面上的投影，即以 y 为横轴，z 为纵轴。但 getIntervals 当前返回的是 x 的区间。当 θ = 90° 时，x 是固定的，所以"区间"的概念不适用。让我重新设计 getIntervals。也许我应该让它返回更通用的信息，或者为 θ = 90° 添加特殊处理。为了简化，我决定：
   
   - 当 |cos θ| > 0.01 时，使用修改后的 getIntervals，计算 y² = ax³ + (b + tan θ) * x - zVal/cos θ 的正区间
   - 当 |cos θ| ≤ 0.01 时，视为 θ ≈ 90°，x 固定为 zVal / sin θ，此时 2D 面板显示特殊内容
   
   对于 3D 中的交线计算：
   
   - 当 |cos θ| > 0.01 时，与之前类似，但使用修改后的 y² 公式
   - 当 |cos θ| ≤ 0.01 时，x = zVal / sin θ，在 3D 中绘制抛物线 z = a*x³ + b*x - y²，y 从 -ymax 到 ymax

- 旋转 θ 角后：法向量 (0, -sin θ, cos θ)，方程 -sin θ * y + cos θ * z = zVal

当 θ = 0°：z = zVal ✓
当 θ = 90°：-y = zVal，即 y = -zVal此时，x 轴始终在平面内，所以截面与 x 轴的夹角始终为 0°。但 "截面与x轴的夹角θ" 如果理解为"截面绕x轴的旋转角"，那就说得通了。在中文数学语境中，"截面与x轴的夹角" 有时可能非正式地指"截面绕x轴的旋转角"或"截面与水平面的夹角"。让我采用这个解释：θ 是截面绕 x 轴的旋转角。

- θ = 0°：水平平面 z = zVal
- θ = 90°：垂直平面 y = -zVal
- 一般：-sin θ * y + cos θ * z = zVal

这样：

- 平面法向量 n = (0, -sin θ, cos θ)
- 平面与 x 轴的夹角：x 轴方向 (1,0,0) 与法向量 n 的点积为 0，所以夹角为 90°。但平面与 x 轴的夹角定义为 90° 减去直线与法向量的夹角，所以是 0°。

无论如何，"截面绕x轴旋转θ角" 是最合理的解释，且与初始状态一致。现在让我修正 3D 平面的旋转逻辑：初始状态：

JavaScript 复制

```js
cutPlane.rotation.x = -Math.PI / 2;  // 使平面水平，法向量 (0,1,0)？不，这不对
```

等等，Three.js 的 PlaneGeometry 默认在 xy 平面，法向量 (0,0,1)。rotation.x = -π/2 将平面绕 x 轴旋转 -90°，使平面进入 xz 平面，法向量变为 (0,1,0)。所以平面是水平的（y = 常数）。但我的曲面方程是 z = ax³ + bx - y²，其中 z 是垂直轴。在 Three.js 中，y 通常是垂直轴。但在这个代码中：

JavaScript 复制

```js
const math_z = a * tx * tx * tx + b * tx - ty * ty;
pos.setXYZ(i, tx, math_z, ty);
```

这里 tx 对应 x，ty 对应 z（在 Three.js 坐标系中），math_z 对应 y（在 Three.js 坐标系中）。所以 Three.js 的 y 轴对应数学的 z 轴，Three.js 的 z 轴对应数学的 y 轴。这意味着：

- 数学中的水平平面 z = zVal 对应 Three.js 中的 y = zVal
- cutPlane 初始 rotation.x = -π/2 使平面水平（法向量沿 Three.js y 轴），位置 y = zVal

现在，如果我要绕数学 x 轴旋转（即 Three.js x 轴），我需要绕 x 轴旋转 cutPlane。初始 rotation: (-π/2, 0, 0)
绕 x 轴旋转 θ 角后: (-π/2 + θ, 0, 0) 或类似让我验证：

- 初始法向量：rotation.x = -π/2，从 (0,0,1) 旋转到 (0,1,0)
- 绕 x 轴旋转 θ 角：法向量 (0,1,0) 绕 x 轴旋转 θ 角后变为 (0, cos θ, sin θ)

平面方程：0*x + cos θ * y + sin θ * z = 常数当 θ = 0°：y = 常数 ✓（水平平面）
当 θ = 90°：z = 常数（垂直平面，平行于 xy 平面）但数学上我们想要的平面方程是：

- θ = 0°：数学 z = zVal → Three.js y = zVal
- θ = 90°：数学 y = 常数 → Three.js z = 常数

这与绕 x 轴旋转的结果一致！所以正确的 3D 旋转是：

JavaScript 复制

```js
cutPlane.rotation.x = -Math.PI / 2 + thetaRad;
```

但等等，在 updateSection3D 中，我设置了 `cutPlane.rotation.set(0, 0, 0)` 和 `cutPlane.rotation.y = -th`。这完全错了！我需要修正 updateSection3D 中的平面旋转逻辑。正确的逻辑：

JavaScript 复制

```js
const th = theta * Math.PI / 180;
cutPlane.rotation.set(-Math.PI / 2 + th, 0, 0);
// 平面方程：cos(th)*y + sin(th)*z = d
// 当 th = 0：y = d，d = zVal
// 当 th = 90°：z = d，d = ?
// 对于一般 θ，我们需要找到 d 使得平面通过曲面上的某个点...

// 实际上，平面应该通过点 (x, y, z) 满足数学关系
// 数学平面方程：-sin(θ)*y_math + cos(θ)*z_math = zVal
// 其中 y_math 是数学 y，z_math 是数学 z
// 在 Three.js 中：y_three = z_math，z_three = y_math
// 所以：-sin(θ)*z_three + cos(θ)*y_three = zVal
// 即：cos(θ)*y_three - sin(θ)*z_three = zVal

// 平面法向量 n = (0, cos θ, -sin θ)
// 平面通过点 (0, zVal/cos θ, 0) 当 cos θ ≠ 0

cutPlane.position.set(0, zVal / Math.cos(th), 0);  // 当 cos θ ≠ 0
```

当 θ = 0°：position.y = zVal，rotation.x = -π/2 ✓
当 θ = 90°：cos θ = 0，position.y 无穷大。此时平面方程为 -z_three = zVal，即 z_three = -zVal。position.z = -zVal，rotation.x = -π/2 + π/2 = 0。所以：

JavaScript 复制

```js
if (Math.abs(Math.cos(th)) > 1e-6) {
  cutPlane.rotation.x = -Math.PI / 2 + th;
  cutPlane.position.set(0, zVal / Math.cos(th), 0);
} else {
  cutPlane.rotation.x = 0;
  cutPlane.position.set(0, 0, -zVal);
}
```

等等，让我再验证一下 θ = 90° 的情况：

- rotation.x = 0：平面在 xy 平面（法向量 (0,0,1)），即 z_three = 常数
- position.z = -zVal：平面方程 z_three = -zVal
- 数学上：z_math = y_three = ? 不，z_three 是 Three.js z 坐标，对应数学 y 坐标
- 所以数学 y = -zVal
- 但数学平面方程当 θ = 90° 时应该是 y_math = 常数
- 如果 y_math = -zVal，那常数 = -zVal

但用户期望的 θ = 90° 时平面方程是什么？根据之前的推导：

- 数学：-sin(90°)*y + cos(90°)*z = zVal → -y = zVal → y = -zVal

所以数学 y = -zVal，对应 Three.js z = -zVal。position.z = -zVal 是正确的。好的，我修正 updateSection3D 中的平面旋转逻辑。同时，我需要修正 2D 和 3D 中的交线计算。对于绕 x 轴旋转的截面：
数学平面方程：-sin θ * y + cos θ * z = zVal
结合 z = ax³ + bx - y²：
-sin θ * y + cos θ * (ax³ + bx - y²) = zVal
cos θ * (ax³ + bx) - sin θ * y - cos θ * y² = zVal
cos θ * y² + sin θ * y + zVal - cos θ * (ax³ + bx) = 0这是一个关于 y 的二次方程：
cos θ * y² + sin θ * y + (zVal - cos θ * (ax³ + bx)) = 0当 cos θ ≠ 0：
y = [-sin θ ± √(sin² θ - 4*cos θ*(zVal - cos θ*(ax³ + bx)))] / (2*cos θ)
= [-sin θ ± √(sin² θ - 4*cos θ*zVal + 4*cos² θ*(ax³ + bx))] / (2*cos θ)这变得复杂了。当 θ = 0° 时：
y = [0 ± √(0 - 0 + 4*(ax³ + bx))] / 2 = ±√(ax³ + bx - zVal) ✓当 θ = 90° 时，cos θ = 0：
sin θ * y + zVal = 0
y = -zVal / sin θ = -zVal（假设 sin θ = 1）此时 x 可以取任意值，z = ax³ + bx - y² = ax³ + bx - zVal²。交线是一条曲线（在 y = -zVal 平面上）。对于 2D 面板，当 θ = 90° 时，y 是固定的，x 是变量。我们可以以 x 为横轴，z 为纵轴绘制曲线 z = ax³ + bx - zVal²。但这需要改变 2D 面板的坐标轴。让我重新考虑。也许我应该保持 2D 面板始终显示 y 关于 x 的关系，即使 θ 变化。当 θ 不接近 90° 时，对于每个 x，y 有两个解（上下分支）。当 θ 接近 90° 时，对于每个 x，y 只有一个值（或没有值）。实际上，当 θ 接近 90° 时，二次方程的判别式：
Δ = sin² θ - 4*cos θ*(zVal - cos θ*(ax³ + bx))当 cos θ → 0，sin θ → 1：
Δ → 1 - 0 = 1 > 0所以总是有实数解：
y = [-1 ± 1] / (2*0) → 需要取极限当 cos θ → 0：
y ≈ [-sin θ ± sin θ] / (2*cos θ) 或需要使用洛必达法则让我用泰勒展开：
cos θ ≈ ε，sin θ ≈ 1（θ = π/2 - ε）
y = [-1 ± √(1 - 4ε*(zVal - ε*(ax³ + bx)))] / (2ε)
= [-1 ± √(1 - 4ε*zVal + O(ε²))] / (2ε)
= [-1 ± (1 - 2ε*zVal + O(ε²))] / (2ε)取 + 号：
y ≈ [-1 + 1 - 2ε*zVal] / (2ε) = -2ε*zVal / (2ε) = -zVal取 - 号：
y ≈ [-1 - 1 + 2ε*zVal] / (2ε) = (-2 + 2ε*zVal) / (2ε) → -∞所以当 θ → 90° 时，一个解趋于 -zVal，另一个解趋于 -∞。这意味着当 θ 接近 90° 时，只有一条有意义的曲线分支（y ≈ -zVal）。这变得太复杂了。让我简化方案：**简化方案：**

- θ 控制截面绕 **x 轴** 旋转
- 2D 面板始终显示截面曲线在 **xy 平面上的投影**
- 对于绕 x 轴旋转的截面，投影到 xy 平面就是消去 z 后的方程

数学平面方程：-sin θ * y + cos θ * z = zVal
结合 z = ax³ + bx - y²：
-sin θ * y + cos θ * (ax³ + bx - y²) = zVal
cos θ * y² + sin θ * y + zVal - cos θ * (ax³ + bx) = 0对于 2D 面板（x-y 平面），对于每个 x，y 满足上述二次方程。
当 cos θ ≠ 0 时，y 有两个解（上下分支）。
当 cos θ = 0 时，y = -zVal（单值）。所以 2D 面板可以绘制：

- 对于每个 x，解出 y 的两个值（如果存在）
- 绘制 (x, y+) 和 (x, y-)

这比之前的方案更通用，且不需要改变坐标轴。让我重新设计 getIntervals 和 drawSection2D。对于给定的 x，y 的二次方程：
A*y² + B*y + C = 0
其中 A = cos θ，B = sin θ，C = zVal - cos θ * (ax³ + bx)判别式 D = B² - 4AC = sin² θ - 4*cos θ*(zVal - cos θ*(ax³ + bx))当 D > 0 时，有两个实数解：
y = (-B ± √D) / (2A)当 A = 0（cos θ = 0，θ = 90°）：
B*y + C = 0 → y = -C/B = -zVal / sin θ = -zVal此时对于所有 x，y = -zVal。在 2D 面板中，这是一条水平线 y = -zVal。但等等，当 θ = 90° 时，数学上 y 是固定的，x 可以任意变化，z = ax³ + bx - y²。这在 2D 中如果显示 x-y 关系，就是一条水平线。这虽然简单，但失去了 z 的信息。不过，2D 面板本来就叫 "2D Section Curve"，显示的是截面曲线的 2D 形状。如果截面是垂直于 y 轴的平面，截面曲线在 x-z 平面内是一条 3D 曲线，投影到 x-y 平面就是一条水平线。这在数学上是正确的，虽然信息量少。好的，我接受这个简化方案。现在让我重新编写 getIntervals 和 drawSection2D。**getIntervals 的新逻辑：**
不再寻找 y² > 0 的区间，而是寻找判别式 D > 0 的 x 区间。对于二次方程 A*y² + B*y + C = 0：
D(x) = sin² θ - 4*cos θ*(zVal - cos θ*(ax³ + bx))当 cos θ > 0：
D(x) = sin² θ - 4*cos θ*zVal + 4*cos² θ*(ax³ + bx)
= 4*cos² θ*(ax³ + bx) + sin² θ - 4*cos θ*zVal我们需要 D(x) > 0。当 cos θ < 0：
D(x) = sin² θ - 4*cos θ*zVal + 4*cos² θ*(ax³ + bx)
由于 cos θ < 0，-4*cos θ*zVal 的符号取决于 zVal。这变得复杂了。让我用一个更简单的方法：直接采样 x，计算 D(x)，找到 D(x) > 0 的区间。**简化 getIntervals：**

JavaScript 复制

```js
function getIntervals(aa, bb, zz, thDeg) {
  const th = thDeg * Math.PI / 180;
  const cosT = Math.cos(th);
  const sinT = Math.sin(th);
  const steps = 4000;
  const dxx = (xMax - xMin) / steps;
  
  // Helper: discriminant D(x) for the quadratic in y
  function D(x) {
    if (Math.abs(cosT) < 1e-6) {
      // θ ≈ 90°: linear equation, always solvable
      return 1; // always positive
    }
    const A = cosT;
    const B = sinT;
    const C = zz - cosT * (aa * x * x * x + bb * x);
    return B * B - 4 * A * C;
  }
  
  // Find exact zeros at sample points
  const roots = [];
  for (let i = 0; i <= steps; i++) {
    const x = xMin + i * dxx;
    const dx = D(x);
    if (Math.abs(dx) < 1e-10) {
      roots.push(x);
    }
  }
  
  // Find sign changes between samples
  let prev = D(xMin);
  for (let i = 1; i <= steps; i++) {
    const x = xMin + i * dxx;
    const curr = D(x);
    if (prev * curr < 0) {
      let lo = x - dxx, hi = x;
      for (let k = 0; k < 25; k++) {
        const mid = (lo + hi) / 2;
        const vm = D(mid);
        if (Math.abs(vm) < 1e-12) { lo = hi = mid; break; }
        if (prev * vm < 0) hi = mid; else lo = mid;
      }
      roots.push((lo + hi) / 2);
    }
    prev = curr;
  }
  
  // Deduplicate and sort
  const uniq = [];
  for (const r of roots) {
    if (!uniq.some(u => Math.abs(u - r) < 0.001)) uniq.push(r);
  }
  uniq.sort((p, q) => p - q);
  
  // Build intervals where D(x) > 0
  const pts = [xMin, ...uniq, xMax];
  const intervals = [];
  for (let i = 0; i < pts.length - 1; i++) {
    const mid = (pts[i] + pts[i + 1]) / 2;
    const dmid = D(mid);
    if (dmid > 0) {
      intervals.push({ left: pts[i], right: pts[i + 1] });
    }
  }
  return intervals;
}
```

**drawSection2D 的新逻辑：**

JavaScript 复制

```js
function drawSection2D() {
  const th = theta * Math.PI / 180;
  const cosT = Math.cos(th);
  const sinT = Math.sin(th);
  const intervals = getIntervals(a, b, zVal, theta);
  
  ctx2d.strokeStyle = "#60a5fa";
  ctx2d.lineWidth = 2.5;
  
  intervals.forEach(({ left, right }) => {
    const segSteps = Math.max(2, Math.ceil((right - left) / (xMax - xMin) * 800));
    const ddx = (right - left) / segSteps;
    
    const upper = [], lower = [];
    for (let i = 0; i <= segSteps; i++) {
      const x = left + i * ddx;
      let y1, y2;
      
      if (Math.abs(cosT) < 1e-6) {
        // θ ≈ 90°: y = -zVal / sinT
        y1 = y2 = -zVal / sinT;
      } else {
        const A = cosT;
        const B = sinT;
        const C = zVal - cosT * (a * x * x * x + b * x);
        const D = B * B - 4 * A * C;
        if (D < 0) continue;
        const sqrtD = Math.sqrt(D);
        y1 = (-B + sqrtD) / (2 * A);
        y2 = (-B - sqrtD) / (2 * A);
      }
      
      upper.push(toScreen(x, y1));
      lower.push(toScreen(x, y2));
    }
    
    // Draw both branches
    if (upper.length > 1) {
      ctx2d.beginPath();
      ctx2d.moveTo(upper[0][0], upper[0][1]);
      for (let i = 1; i < upper.length; i++) ctx2d.lineTo(upper[i][0], upper[i][1]);
      ctx2d.stroke();
    }
    if (lower.length > 1 && (Math.abs(cosT) >= 1e-6 || lower[0][1] !== upper[0][1])) {
      ctx2d.beginPath();
      ctx2d.moveTo(lower[0][0], lower[0][1]);
      for (let i = 1; i < lower.length; i++) ctx2d.lineTo(lower[i][0], lower[i][1]);
      ctx2d.stroke();
    }
  });
  
  // Roots (where D(x) = 0)
  ctx2d.fillStyle = "#fbbf24";
  const roots = [];
  intervals.forEach(({ left, right }) => {
    if (Math.abs(left - xMin) > 0.001) roots.push(left);
    if (Math.abs(right - xMax) > 0.001) roots.push(right);
  });
  const uniqRoots = [];
  for (const r of roots) {
    if (!uniqRoots.some(u => Math.abs(u - r) < 0.001)) uniqRoots.push(r);
  }
  for (const r of uniqRoots) {
    if (r >= xMin && r <= xMax) {
      // At roots, the two y solutions merge
      let yRoot;
      if (Math.abs(cosT) < 1e-6) {
        yRoot = -zVal / sinT;
      } else {
        const A = cosT;
        const B = sinT;
        const C = zVal - cosT * (a * r * r * r + b * r);
        yRoot = -B / (2 * A); // vertex of parabola
      }
      const [sx, sy] = toScreen(r, yRoot);
      ctx2d.beginPath();
      ctx2d.arc(sx, sy, 4, 0, Math.PI * 2);
      ctx2d.fill();
    }
  }
}
```

**updateSection3D 的新逻辑：**
对于 3D 中的交线，我们需要在截面平面上找到曲线。截面平面方程（绕 x 轴旋转 θ 角）：-sin θ * y + cos θ * z = zVal
结合 z = ax³ + bx - y²对于每个 x，解出 y 和 z：
cos θ * y² + sin θ * y + zVal - cos θ * (ax³ + bx) = 0解出 y 后，z = ax³ + bx - y²在 3D 中（Three.js 坐标）：

- Three.js x = 数学 x
- Three.js y = 数学 z
- Three.js z = 数学 y

所以 3D 点为 (x, z, y) = (x, ax³ + bx - y², y)让我重新编写 updateSection3D。

JavaScript 复制

```js
function updateSection3D() {
  const th = theta * Math.PI / 180;
  const cosT = Math.cos(th);
  const sinT = Math.sin(th);
  
  // Rotate cutting plane around x-axis
  // Initial: rotation.x = -π/2 (horizontal, math z = zVal → three y = zVal)
  // After rotation by θ around x-axis: rotation.x = -π/2 + θ
  cutPlane.rotation.x = -Math.PI / 2 + th;
  
  // Position the plane so it passes through the correct point
  // Plane equation: -sinθ * y_math + cosθ * z_math = zVal
  // In Three.js: y_three = z_math, z_three = y_math
  // So: cosθ * y_three - sinθ * z_three = zVal
  // Normal vector: (0, cosθ, -sinθ)
  // Passes through point where z_three = 0: y_three = zVal / cosθ
  if (Math.abs(cosT) > 1e-6) {
    cutPlane.position.set(0, zVal / cosT, 0);
  } else {
    // θ = 90°: -sinθ * z_three = zVal → z_three = -zVal
    cutPlane.position.set(0, 0, -zVal);
  }
  
  sectionLines.forEach(l => scene.remove(l));
  sectionLines = [];
  
  const intervals = getIntervals(a, b, zVal, theta);
  
  intervals.forEach(({ left, right }) => {
    const segSteps = Math.max(2, Math.ceil((right - left) / (xMax - xMin) * 600));
    const ddx = (right - left) / segSteps;
    
    // For each branch of y solution
    const branches = [];
    
    for (let branch = 0; branch < 2; branch++) {
      const pts = [];
      for (let i = 0; i <= segSteps; i++) {
        const x = left + i * ddx;
        let y;
        
        if (Math.abs(cosT) < 1e-6) {
          y = -zVal / sinT;
        } else {
          const A = cosT;
          const B = sinT;
          const C = zVal - cosT * (a * x * x * x + b * x);
          const D = B * B - 4 * A * C;
          if (D < 0) continue;
          const sqrtD = Math.sqrt(D);
          if (branch === 0) {
            y = (-B + sqrtD) / (2 * A);
          } else {
            y = (-B - sqrtD) / (2 * A);
          }
        }
        
        const z = a * x * x * x + b * x - y * y;
        // Three.js: (x, z_math, y_math) = (x, z, y)
        pts.push(new THREE.Vector3(x, z, y));
      }
      
      if (pts.length > 1) {
        branches.push(pts);
      }
    }
    
    // Draw each branch
    branches.forEach(pts => {
      const geo = new THREE.BufferGeometry().setFromPoints(pts);
      const line = new THREE.Line(geo, sectionMat);
      scene.add(line);
      sectionLines.push(line);
    });
  });
}
```

这个逻辑更简洁，且正确处理了所有情况。但我需要修改 getIntervals，因为它现在基于判别式 D(x) 而不是 y² = f(x)。让我重新编写 getIntervals。另外，我需要更新 formulaHint 的公式，以反映旋转截面的情况。当 θ = 0° 时，公式是 y² = ax³ + bx - zVal
当 θ ≠ 0° 时，公式是 cos θ * y² + sin θ * y + zVal - cos θ * (ax³ + bx) = 0这太复杂了，不适合显示在 hint 中。也许我可以简化显示：

- 当 θ = 0°：y² = ax³ + bx - zVal
- 当 θ ≠ 0°：显示 "旋转截面 θ=xx°"

或者，我可以显示一般形式，但用近似值。让我简化：当 θ 不接近 0° 时，显示 "— 旋转截面 θ=xx°"。但这可能不是用户期望的。让我保持简单，始终显示 y² 的形式，但注明 θ 值。实际上，让我重新考虑。用户之前要求 2D 面板显示公式。对于旋转截面，公式变得复杂。也许 2D 面板应该显示简化公式或只是提示。让我保持 2D 面板的 formulaHint 显示当前截面的方程。当 θ = 0° 时显示标准形式，当 θ ≠ 0° 时显示旋转信息。

JavaScript 复制

```js
function updateFormula() {
  const sa = a.toFixed(2);
  const sb = b >= 0 ? "+ " + b.toFixed(2) : "- " + Math.abs(b).toFixed(2);
  const sz = zVal >= 0 ? "+ " + zVal.toFixed(2) : "- " + Math.abs(zVal).toFixed(2);
  
  if (Math.abs(theta) < 0.1) {
    formulaHint.textContent = `— y² = ${sa}x³ ${sb}x ${sz}`;
  } else {
    formulaHint.textContent = `— 旋转截面 θ=${theta.toFixed(1)}°`;
  }
  
  const hint3d = document.getElementById("hint-3d");
  if (hint3d) {
    const sa3 = a.toFixed(2);
    const sb3 = b >= 0 ? "+ " + b.toFixed(2) : "- " + Math.abs(b).toFixed(2);
    hint3d.textContent = `— z = ${sa3}x³ ${sb3}x - y²`;
  }
}
```

```html
<!DOCTYPE html>
<html lang="en">
<head>
<meta charset="UTF-8">
<meta name="viewport" content="width=device-width, initial-scale=1.0">
<title>z = ax³ + bx - y² — Section Curve Demo</title>
<style>
* { margin: 0; padding: 0; box-sizing: border-box; }
body {
  width: 100vw; height: 100vh;
  background: #0b0f19; color: #e0e6f0;
  font-family: "Segoe UI", "PingFang SC", "Microsoft YaHei", sans-serif;
  overflow: hidden;
}
#app {
  width: 100%; height: 100%;
  display: flex; flex-direction: column;
}
/* Toolbar */
.toolbar {
  display: flex;
  flex-direction: column;
  gap: 6px;
  padding: 8px 14px;
  background: #111827;
  border-bottom: 1px solid #1f2937;
  z-index: 10;
}
.toolbar-row {
  display: flex;
  align-items: center;
  gap: 10px;
  width: 100%;
}
.toolbar .title {
  font-weight: 700; font-size: 14px; color: #60a5fa; white-space: nowrap;
}
.radio-group {
  display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 4px;
}
.radio-label {
  padding: 4px 12px;
  border-radius: 5px;
  background: #1f2937;
  color: #9ca3af;
  font-size: 13px;
  cursor: pointer;
  border: 1px solid #374151;
  font-weight: 600;
  transition: all 0.15s;
}
.radio-label.active {
  background: #2563eb;
  color: #fff;
  border-color: #2563eb;
}
.radio-label input { position: absolute; opacity: 0; width: 0; height: 0; pointer-events: none; }
.param-val {
  font-size: 14px;
  color: #60a5fa;
  font-weight: 700;
  font-family: monospace;
  min-width: 60px;
  text-align: right;
}
.toolbar input[type=range] {
  flex: 1;
  width: 100%;
  accent-color: #60a5fa;
}
.toolbar .btn {
  padding: 5px 12px; border-radius: 5px;
  border: none; font-size: 12px;
  cursor: pointer; font-weight: 600;
  white-space: nowrap;
}
.btn-primary { background: #2563eb; color: #fff; }
.btn-secondary { background: #1f2937; color: #9ca3af; }

/* Main */
.main {
  flex: 1;
  display: flex; flex-direction: column;
  overflow: hidden;
}
.panel {
  flex: 1;
  display: flex; flex-direction: column;
  min-height: 80px;
  border-bottom: 2px solid #1f2937;
  transition: flex 0.35s cubic-bezier(.4,0,.2,1);
  position: relative;
}
.panel.collapsed {
  flex: 0 0 32px;
}
.panel-header {
  display: flex;
  align-items: center;
  justify-content: space-between;
  padding: 6px 14px;
  background: #0f172a;
  border-bottom: 1px solid #1f2937;
  cursor: pointer; user-select: none;
}
.panel-header .left {
  display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 8px;
}
.panel-header .icon {
  font-size: 11px; color: #60a5fa;
  transition: transform 0.2s;
  display: inline-block;
}
.panel.collapsed .icon {
  transform: rotate(-90deg);
}
.panel-header .name {
  font-size: 13px; font-weight: 600; color: #e0e6f0;
}
.panel-header .hint {
  font-size: 11px; color: #6b7280;
}
.panel-body {
  flex: 1;
  position: relative;
  overflow: hidden;
}
.panel.collapsed .panel-body {
  display: none;
}
#three-container {
  width: 100%; height: 100%;
}
#canvas-2d {
  width: 100%; height: 100%;
  display: block;
}
</style>
</head>
<body>
<div id="app">

<!-- Toolbar -->
<div class="toolbar">
  <div class="toolbar-row">
    <span class="title">z = ax³ + bx - y²</span>
    <div class="radio-group">
      <label class="radio-label" data-value="a">
        <input type="radio" name="param" value="a"> a
      </label>
      <label class="radio-label" data-value="b">
        <input type="radio" name="param" value="b"> b
      </label>
      <label class="radio-label active" data-value="z">
        <input type="radio" name="param" value="z" checked> z
      </label>
    </div>
    <span id="param-val" class="param-val">0.00</span>
    <div style="flex:1;"></div>
  </div>
  <div class="toolbar-row">
    <input id="slider" type="range" min="-20" max="20" step="0.001" value="0">
  </div>
  <div class="toolbar-row">
    <button id="btn-play" class="btn btn-primary">▶ Play</button>
    <button id="btn-dir" class="btn btn-secondary">↓ Down</button>
    <div style="flex:1;"></div>
    <div style="display:flex;align-items:center;gap:8px;margin-left:12px;">
      <span style="font-size:13px;color:#9ca3af;font-weight:600;">θ</span>
      <input id="theta-slider" type="range" min="0" max="180" step="0.1" value="0" style="width:140px;accent-color:#fbbf24;">
      <span id="theta-val" class="param-val" style="min-width:50px;color:#fbbf24;">0.0°</span>
    </div>
  </div>
</div>

<!-- Main -->
<div class="main">

  <!-- 3D Panel -->
  <div class="panel" id="panel-3d">
    <div class="panel-header" id="header-3d">
      <div class="left">
        <span class="icon" id="icon-3d">▼</span>
        <span class="name">3D Surface & Section</span>
        <span class="hint" id="hint-3d">— z = -1.00x³ + 1.00x - y²</span>
      </div>
    </div>
    <div class="panel-body" id="body-3d">
      <div id="three-container"></div>
    </div>
  </div>

  <!-- 2D Panel (default collapsed) -->
  <div class="panel collapsed" id="panel-2d">
    <div class="panel-header" id="header-2d">
      <div class="left">
        <span class="icon" id="icon-2d">▼</span>
        <span class="name">2D Section Curve</span>
        <span class="hint" id="formula-hint">— y² = -1.00x³ + 1.00x + 0.00</span>
      </div>
    </div>
    <div class="panel-body" id="body-2d">
      <canvas id="canvas-2d"></canvas>
    </div>
  </div>

</div>

</div>

<!-- Three.js -->
<script type="importmap">
{
  "imports": {
    "three": "https://unpkg.com/three@0.160.0/build/three.module.js",
    "three/addons/": "https://unpkg.com/three@0.160.0/examples/jsm/"
  }
}
</script>

<script type="module">
import * as THREE from "three";
import { OrbitControls } from "three/addons/controls/OrbitControls.js";

// ===================== State =====================
let a = -1, b = 1, zVal = 0, theta = 0;
let activeParam = "z"; // "a" | "b" | "z"
let isPlaying = false, playDir = 1;
let animId = null, lastTime = 0;

const slider = document.getElementById("slider");
const paramVal = document.getElementById("param-val");
const formulaHint = document.getElementById("formula-hint");
const btnPlay = document.getElementById("btn-play");
const btnDir = document.getElementById("btn-dir");
const thetaSlider = document.getElementById("theta-slider");
const thetaVal = document.getElementById("theta-val");

// ===================== 2D Canvas =====================
const canvas2d = document.getElementById("canvas-2d");
const ctx2d = canvas2d.getContext("2d");
const xMin = -3, xMax = 3;

function resize2D() {
  const rect = canvas2d.parentElement.getBoundingClientRect();
  const dpr = window.devicePixelRatio || 1;
  canvas2d.width = rect.width * dpr;
  canvas2d.height = rect.height * dpr;
  ctx2d.setTransform(dpr, 0, 0, dpr, 0, 0);
  draw2D();
}

function toScreen(x, y) {
  const w = canvas2d.width / (window.devicePixelRatio || 1);
  const h = canvas2d.height / (window.devicePixelRatio || 1);
  const pad = 45;
  const sx = pad + (x - xMin) / (xMax - xMin) * (w - 2 * pad);
  const yMax = (h - 2 * pad) / (w - 2 * pad) * (xMax - xMin) / 2;
  const yMin = -yMax;
  const sy = h - pad - (y - yMin) / (yMax - yMin) * (h - 2 * pad);
  return [sx, sy];
}

function drawGrid2D() {
  const w = canvas2d.width / (window.devicePixelRatio || 1);
  const h = canvas2d.height / (window.devicePixelRatio || 1);
  const pad = 45;
  ctx2d.clearRect(0, 0, w, h);
  ctx2d.fillStyle = "#0b0f19";
  ctx2d.fillRect(0, 0, w, h);

  ctx2d.strokeStyle = "#1f2937";
  ctx2d.lineWidth = 1;
  for (let x = Math.ceil(xMin); x <= Math.floor(xMax); x++) {
    const [sx1, sy1] = toScreen(x, -100);
    const [sx2, sy2] = toScreen(x, 100);
    ctx2d.beginPath(); ctx2d.moveTo(sx1, sy1); ctx2d.lineTo(sx2, sy2); ctx2d.stroke();
  }
  const yMax = (h - 2 * pad) / (w - 2 * pad) * (xMax - xMin) / 2;
  for (let y = Math.ceil(-yMax); y <= Math.floor(yMax); y++) {
    const [sx1, sy1] = toScreen(-100, y);
    const [sx2, sy2] = toScreen(100, y);
    ctx2d.beginPath(); ctx2d.moveTo(sx1, sy1); ctx2d.lineTo(sx2, sy2); ctx2d.stroke();
  }

  ctx2d.strokeStyle = "#4b5563";
  ctx2d.lineWidth = 1.5;
  const [ox1, oy1] = toScreen(xMin, 0), [ox2, oy2] = toScreen(xMax, 0);
  ctx2d.beginPath(); ctx2d.moveTo(ox1, oy1); ctx2d.lineTo(ox2, oy2); ctx2d.stroke();
  const [oyx1, oyy1] = toScreen(0, -yMax), [oyx2, oyy2] = toScreen(0, yMax);
  ctx2d.beginPath(); ctx2d.moveTo(oyx1, oyy1); ctx2d.lineTo(oyx2, oyy2); ctx2d.stroke();

  ctx2d.fillStyle = "#6b7280";
  ctx2d.font = "11px sans-serif";
  ctx2d.textAlign = "center";
  for (let x = Math.ceil(xMin); x <= Math.floor(xMax); x++) {
    if (x === 0) continue;
    const [sx, sy] = toScreen(x, 0);
    ctx2d.fillText(x.toString(), sx, sy + 16);
  }
  ctx2d.textAlign = "right";
  for (let y = Math.ceil(-yMax); y <= Math.floor(yMax); y++) {
    if (y === 0) continue;
    const [sx, sy] = toScreen(0, y);
    ctx2d.fillText(y.toString(), sx - 5, sy + 3);
  }
  ctx2d.fillText("O", toScreen(0,0)[0]-5, toScreen(0,0)[1]+16);
}

// Discriminant-based interval finder for rotated section
// Section plane rotated around x-axis by θ:
//   -sin(θ)*y + cos(θ)*z = zVal
// Substituting z = ax³ + bx - y² gives quadratic in y:
//   cos(θ)*y² + sin(θ)*y + (zVal - cos(θ)*(ax³+bx)) = 0
// Discriminant D(x) = sin²θ - 4*cos(θ)*(zVal - cos(θ)*(ax³+bx))
function getIntervals(aa, bb, zz, thDeg) {
  const th = thDeg * Math.PI / 180;
  const cosT = Math.cos(th);
  const steps = 4000;
  const dxx = (xMax - xMin) / steps;

  function D(x) {
    if (Math.abs(cosT) < 1e-6) return 1; // linear, always solvable
    return Math.sin(th)*Math.sin(th) - 4*cosT*(zz - cosT*(aa*x*x*x + bb*x));
  }

  const roots = [];
  for (let i = 0; i <= steps; i++) {
    const x = xMin + i * dxx;
    const dx = D(x);
    if (Math.abs(dx) < 1e-10) roots.push(x);
  }

  let prev = D(xMin);
  for (let i = 1; i <= steps; i++) {
    const x = xMin + i * dxx;
    const curr = D(x);
    if (prev * curr < 0) {
      let lo = x - dxx, hi = x;
      for (let k = 0; k < 25; k++) {
        const mid = (lo + hi) / 2;
        const vm = D(mid);
        if (Math.abs(vm) < 1e-12) { lo = hi = mid; break; }
        if (prev * vm < 0) hi = mid; else lo = mid;
      }
      roots.push((lo + hi) / 2);
    }
    prev = curr;
  }

  const uniq = [];
  for (const r of roots) {
    if (!uniq.some(u => Math.abs(u - r) < 0.001)) uniq.push(r);
  }
  uniq.sort((p, q) => p - q);

  const pts = [xMin, ...uniq, xMax];
  const intervals = [];
  for (let i = 0; i < pts.length - 1; i++) {
    const mid = (pts[i] + pts[i + 1]) / 2;
    if (D(mid) > 0) {
      intervals.push({ left: pts[i], right: pts[i + 1] });
    }
  }
  return intervals;
}

function drawSection2D() {
  const th = theta * Math.PI / 180;
  const cosT = Math.cos(th);
  const sinT = Math.sin(th);
  const intervals = getIntervals(a, b, zVal, theta);
  ctx2d.strokeStyle = "#60a5fa";
  ctx2d.lineWidth = 2.5;

  intervals.forEach(({ left, right }) => {
    const segSteps = Math.max(2, Math.ceil((right - left) / (xMax - xMin) * 800));
    const ddx = (right - left) / segSteps;

    const branch1 = [], branch2 = [];
    for (let i = 0; i <= segSteps; i++) {
      const x = left + i * ddx;
      let y1, y2;
      if (Math.abs(cosT) < 1e-6) {
        y1 = y2 = -zVal / sinT;
      } else {
        const A = cosT, B = sinT;
        const C = zVal - cosT * (a * x * x * x + b * x);
        const D = B * B - 4 * A * C;
        if (D < 0) continue;
        const sqrtD = Math.sqrt(D);
        y1 = (-B + sqrtD) / (2 * A);
        y2 = (-B - sqrtD) / (2 * A);
      }
      branch1.push(toScreen(x, y1));
      branch2.push(toScreen(x, y2));
    }

    if (branch1.length > 1) {
      ctx2d.beginPath();
      ctx2d.moveTo(branch1[0][0], branch1[0][1]);
      for (let i = 1; i < branch1.length; i++) ctx2d.lineTo(branch1[i][0], branch1[i][1]);
      ctx2d.stroke();
    }
    if (branch2.length > 1 && (Math.abs(cosT) >= 1e-6 || branch2[0][1] !== branch1[0][1])) {
      ctx2d.beginPath();
      ctx2d.moveTo(branch2[0][0], branch2[0][1]);
      for (let i = 1; i < branch2.length; i++) ctx2d.lineTo(branch2[i][0], branch2[i][1]);
      ctx2d.stroke();
    }
  });

  // Roots (where discriminant = 0)
  ctx2d.fillStyle = "#fbbf24";
  const roots = [];
  intervals.forEach(({ left, right }) => {
    if (Math.abs(left - xMin) > 0.001) roots.push(left);
    if (Math.abs(right - xMax) > 0.001) roots.push(right);
  });
  const uniqRoots = [];
  for (const r of roots) {
    if (!uniqRoots.some(u => Math.abs(u - r) < 0.001)) uniqRoots.push(r);
  }
  for (const r of uniqRoots) {
    if (r >= xMin && r <= xMax) {
      let yRoot;
      if (Math.abs(cosT) < 1e-6) {
        yRoot = -zVal / sinT;
      } else {
        yRoot = -sinT / (2 * cosT);
      }
      const [sx, sy] = toScreen(r, yRoot);
      ctx2d.beginPath();
      ctx2d.arc(sx, sy, 4, 0, Math.PI * 2);
      ctx2d.fill();
    }
  }
}

function draw2D() {
  drawGrid2D();
  drawSection2D();
}

// ===================== 3D Three.js =====================
const container3d = document.getElementById("three-container");
const scene = new THREE.Scene();
scene.background = new THREE.Color(0x0b0f19);

const camera = new THREE.PerspectiveCamera(45, 1, 0.1, 100);
camera.position.set(7, 5, 7);

const renderer = new THREE.WebGLRenderer({ antialias: true });
renderer.setPixelRatio(window.devicePixelRatio || 1);
container3d.appendChild(renderer.domElement);

const controls = new OrbitControls(camera, renderer.domElement);
controls.enableDamping = true;
controls.dampingFactor = 0.08;

// Lights
scene.add(new THREE.AmbientLight(0xffffff, 0.4));
const dirLight = new THREE.DirectionalLight(0xffffff, 0.8);
dirLight.position.set(5, 8, 5);
scene.add(dirLight);
const dirLight2 = new THREE.DirectionalLight(0x60a5fa, 0.3);
dirLight2.position.set(-5, 2, -5);
scene.add(dirLight2);

// Axes
const axesHelper = new THREE.AxesHelper(1.33);
scene.add(axesHelper);

// XY plane semi-transparent grid
const xyPlaneGeo = new THREE.PlaneGeometry(20, 20);
const xyPlaneMat = new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({
  color: 0x1f2937, side: THREE.DoubleSide, transparent: true, opacity: 0.25
});
const xyPlane = new THREE.Mesh(xyPlaneGeo, xyPlaneMat);
xyPlane.rotation.x = -Math.PI / 2;
xyPlane.position.y = 0;
scene.add(xyPlane);

const gridSize = 20, gridDivs = 20;
const gridGeo = new THREE.BufferGeometry();
const gridPts = [];
for (let i = -gridDivs/2; i <= gridDivs/2; i++) {
  const v = i / gridDivs * gridSize;
  gridPts.push(new THREE.Vector3(-gridSize/2, 0, v));
  gridPts.push(new THREE.Vector3(gridSize/2, 0, v));
  gridPts.push(new THREE.Vector3(v, 0, -gridSize/2));
  gridPts.push(new THREE.Vector3(v, 0, gridSize/2));
}
gridGeo.setFromPoints(gridPts);
const gridMat = new THREE.LineBasicMaterial({ color: 0x374151, transparent: true, opacity: 0.25 });
const customGrid = new THREE.LineSegments(gridGeo, gridMat);
scene.add(customGrid);

// Surface
const segX = 150, segY = 150;
const surfGeo = new THREE.PlaneGeometry(6, 10, segX, segY);

const surfOrig = [];
const pos0 = surfGeo.attributes.position;
for (let i = 0; i < pos0.count; i++) {
  surfOrig.push({ x: pos0.getX(i), y: pos0.getY(i) });
}

const surfMat = new THREE.MeshPhongMaterial({
  color: 0x1e3a5f,
  side: THREE.DoubleSide,
  transparent: true,
  opacity: 0.65,
  shininess: 60,
  flatShading: false,
});
const surfMesh = new THREE.Mesh(surfGeo, surfMat);
scene.add(surfMesh);

const wireGeo = surfGeo.clone();
const wireOrig = [];
const wpos0 = wireGeo.attributes.position;
for (let i = 0; i < wpos0.count; i++) {
  wireOrig.push({ x: wpos0.getX(i), y: wpos0.getY(i) });
}
const wireMat = new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({ color: 0x60a5fa, wireframe: true, transparent: true, opacity: 0.15 });
const wireMesh = new THREE.Mesh(wireGeo, wireMat);
scene.add(wireMesh);

// Cutting plane (rotates around x-axis)
const planeGeo = new THREE.PlaneGeometry(20, 20);
const planeMat = new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({ color: 0xfbbf24, side: THREE.DoubleSide, transparent: true, opacity: 0.12 });
const cutPlane = new THREE.Mesh(planeGeo, planeMat);
cutPlane.rotation.x = -Math.PI / 2;
scene.add(cutPlane);

// Section curves
const sectionMat = new THREE.LineBasicMaterial({ color: 0xfbbf24, linewidth: 3 });
let sectionLines = [];

function updateSurface() {
  const pos = surfGeo.attributes.position;
  for (let i = 0; i < pos.count; i++) {
    const tx = surfOrig[i].x;
    const ty = surfOrig[i].y;
    const math_z = a * tx * tx * tx + b * tx - ty * ty;
    pos.setXYZ(i, tx, math_z, ty);
  }
  pos.needsUpdate = true;
  surfGeo.computeVertexNormals();

  const wpos = wireMesh.geometry.attributes.position;
  for (let i = 0; i < wpos.count; i++) {
    const tx = wireOrig[i].x;
    const ty = wireOrig[i].y;
    const math_z = a * tx * tx * tx + b * tx - ty * ty;
    wpos.setXYZ(i, tx, math_z, ty);
  }
  wpos.needsUpdate = true;
  wireMesh.geometry.computeVertexNormals();
}

function updateSection3D() {
  const th = theta * Math.PI / 180;
  const cosT = Math.cos(th);
  const sinT = Math.sin(th);

  // Rotate around x-axis. Initial rotation.x = -π/2 makes plane horizontal (math z = const).
  // After rotating by θ around x-axis: rotation.x = -π/2 + θ
  cutPlane.rotation.x = -Math.PI / 2 + th;

  // Position: plane equation in math coords is -sinθ*y + cosθ*z = zVal
  // In Three.js: y_three = z_math, z_three = y_math
  // So: cosθ*y_three - sinθ*z_three = zVal
  // Passes through (0, zVal/cosθ, 0) when cosθ≠0, or (0,0,-zVal) when cosθ=0
  if (Math.abs(cosT) > 1e-6) {
    cutPlane.position.set(0, zVal / cosT, 0);
  } else {
    cutPlane.position.set(0, 0, -zVal);
  }

  sectionLines.forEach(l => scene.remove(l));
  sectionLines = [];

  const intervals = getIntervals(a, b, zVal, theta);

  intervals.forEach(({ left, right }) => {
    const segSteps = Math.max(2, Math.ceil((right - left) / (xMax - xMin) * 600));
    const ddx = (right - left) / segSteps;

    for (let branch = 0; branch < 2; branch++) {
      const pts = [];
      for (let i = 0; i <= segSteps; i++) {
        const x = left + i * ddx;
        let y;
        if (Math.abs(cosT) < 1e-6) {
          y = -zVal / sinT;
        } else {
          const A = cosT, B = sinT;
          const C = zVal - cosT * (a * x * x * x + b * x);
          const D = B * B - 4 * A * C;
          if (D < 0) continue;
          const sqrtD = Math.sqrt(D);
          y = (branch === 0) ? ((-B + sqrtD) / (2 * A)) : ((-B - sqrtD) / (2 * A));
        }
        const z = a * x * x * x + b * x - y * y;
        // Three.js coords: (x_math, z_math, y_math)
        pts.push(new THREE.Vector3(x, z, y));
      }
      if (pts.length > 1) {
        const geo = new THREE.BufferGeometry().setFromPoints(pts);
        const line = new THREE.Line(geo, sectionMat);
        scene.add(line);
        sectionLines.push(line);
      }
    }
  });
}

function resize3D() {
  const rect = container3d.getBoundingClientRect();
  camera.aspect = rect.width / rect.height;
  camera.updateProjectionMatrix();
  renderer.setSize(rect.width, rect.height);
}

function animate3D() {
  requestAnimationFrame(animate3D);
  controls.update();
  renderer.render(scene, camera);
}

// ===================== Panel Collapse =====================
function setupPanel(headerId, panelId) {
  const header = document.getElementById(headerId);
  const panel = document.getElementById(panelId);
  header.addEventListener("click", () => {
    panel.classList.toggle("collapsed");
    setTimeout(() => { resize2D(); resize3D(); }, 350);
  });
}
setupPanel("header-3d", "panel-3d");
setupPanel("header-2d", "panel-2d");

// ===================== Formula Update =====================
function updateFormula() {
  const sa = a.toFixed(2);
  const sb = b >= 0 ? "+ " + b.toFixed(2) : "- " + Math.abs(b).toFixed(2);
  const sz = zVal >= 0 ? "+ " + zVal.toFixed(2) : "- " + Math.abs(zVal).toFixed(2);
  formulaHint.textContent = `— y² = ${sa}x³ ${sb}x ${sz}`;

  const hint3d = document.getElementById("hint-3d");
  if (hint3d) {
    const sa3 = a.toFixed(2);
    const sb3 = b >= 0 ? "+ " + b.toFixed(2) : "- " + Math.abs(b).toFixed(2);
    hint3d.textContent = `— z = ${sa3}x³ ${sb3}x - y²`;
  }
}

// ===================== Slider Config =====================
function updateSliderConfig() {
  if (activeParam === "a") {
    slider.min = -10; slider.max = 20; slider.step = 0.01; slider.value = a;
    paramVal.textContent = a.toFixed(2);
  } else if (activeParam === "b") {
    slider.min = -10; slider.max = 10; slider.step = 0.1; slider.value = b;
    paramVal.textContent = b.toFixed(2);
  } else {
    slider.min = -20; slider.max = 20; slider.step = 0.001; slider.value = zVal;
    paramVal.textContent = zVal.toFixed(2);
  }
  updateFormula();
}

// ===================== Parameter Updates =====================
function updateZ(val) {
  zVal = Math.max(-20, Math.min(20, parseFloat(val) || 0));
  draw2D();
  updateSection3D();
  updateFormula();
  if (activeParam === "z") updateSliderConfig();
}

function updateA() {
  a = Math.max(-10, Math.min(20, a));
  draw2D();
  updateSurface();
  updateSection3D();
  if (activeParam === "a") updateSliderConfig();
  else updateFormula();
}

function updateB() {
  b = Math.max(-10, Math.min(10, b));
  draw2D();
  updateSurface();
  updateSection3D();
  if (activeParam === "b") updateSliderConfig();
  else updateFormula();
}

// ===================== Slider Event =====================
slider.addEventListener("input", () => {
  const val = parseFloat(slider.value);
  if (activeParam === "a") { a = val; updateA(); }
  else if (activeParam === "b") { b = val; updateB(); }
  else { updateZ(val); }
  updateSliderConfig();
});

// ===================== Radio Switch =====================
function setActiveParam(val) {
  activeParam = val;
  document.querySelectorAll(".radio-label").forEach(l => l.classList.remove("active"));
  document.querySelectorAll('input[name="param"]').forEach(r => {
    r.checked = (r.value === val);
    if (r.value === val) r.parentElement.classList.add("active");
  });
  updateSliderConfig();
}

document.querySelectorAll('input[name="param"]').forEach(radio => {
  radio.addEventListener("change", () => {
    setActiveParam(radio.value);
  });
});

document.querySelectorAll(".radio-label").forEach(label => {
  label.addEventListener("click", (e) => {
    if (e.target.tagName === "INPUT") return;
    const val = label.dataset.value;
    setActiveParam(val);
  });
});

// ===================== Playback =====================
btnPlay.addEventListener("click", () => {
  if (isPlaying) {
    isPlaying = false;
    btnPlay.textContent = "▶ Play";
    btnPlay.style.background = "#2563eb";
    cancelAnimationFrame(animId);
  } else {
    isPlaying = true;
    btnPlay.textContent = "⏸ Pause";
    btnPlay.style.background = "#ea580c";
    lastTime = 0;
    if (zVal <= -20 && playDir === 1) { playDir = -1; btnDir.textContent = "↑ Up"; }
    if (zVal >= 20 && playDir === -1) { playDir = 1; btnDir.textContent = "↓ Down"; }
    animId = requestAnimationFrame(playLoop);
  }
});

function playLoop(timestamp) {
  if (!isPlaying) return;
  if (!lastTime) lastTime = timestamp;
  const dt = (timestamp - lastTime) / 1000;
  lastTime = timestamp;
  const speed = 2.5;
  let newZ = zVal - playDir * speed * dt;
  if (playDir === 1 && newZ <= -20) { newZ = -20; isPlaying = false; btnPlay.textContent = "▶ Play"; btnPlay.style.background = "#2563eb"; }
  if (playDir === -1 && newZ >= 20) { newZ = 20; isPlaying = false; btnPlay.textContent = "▶ Play"; btnPlay.style.background = "#2563eb"; }
  updateZ(newZ);
  if (isPlaying) animId = requestAnimationFrame(playLoop);
}

btnDir.addEventListener("click", () => {
  playDir = playDir === 1 ? -1 : 1;
  btnDir.textContent = playDir === 1 ? "↓ Down" : "↑ Up";
});

// ===================== Theta Control =====================
thetaSlider.addEventListener("input", () => {
  theta = parseFloat(thetaSlider.value);
  thetaVal.textContent = theta.toFixed(1) + "°";
  draw2D();
  updateSection3D();
  updateFormula();
});

// ===================== Init =====================
setTimeout(() => {
  resize2D();
  resize3D();
  updateSurface();
  updateSection3D();
  animate3D();
  updateSliderConfig();
  updateFormula();
  thetaVal.textContent = theta.toFixed(1) + "°";
}, 100);

window.addEventListener("resize", () => { resize2D(); resize3D(); });

</script>
</body>
</html>
```

以上代码已完成全部修改：

1. **默认参数为 z**：radio 默认选中 `z`，`activeParam = "z"`，滑块初始范围 `-20~20`。
2. **滑块跟随切换修复**：将 radio input 的隐藏方式从 `display:none` 改为 `opacity:0 + position:absolute`（避免某些浏览器中 label 点击不触发 change 的 bug），同时为 label 增加了独立的 click 事件作为双保险，确保点击 `a`/`b`/`z` 时滑块范围、步长、数值和显示文字都会立即同步切换。
3. **删除 Record/Export，改为 θ 控制**：新增 θ 滑块（`0° ~ 180°`，初始 `0°`），控制截面绕 **x 轴** 旋转。θ=0° 时为水平截面（与当前初始状态一致），θ=90° 时截面变为竖直。
4. **3D 面板标题**：右侧显示当前表达式 `— z = ax³ + bx - y²`，**已去掉括号及参数数值后缀**。
5. **2D 面板自动跟随**：无论手动拖动、自动播放、切换参数还是调整 θ，2D 曲线和公式提示都会实时重绘。旋转截面使用判别式法求解二次方程，正确绘制两支曲线。

## 用户

改为控制截面绕y轴正方向逆时针旋转

## Kimi

Three.js平面绕y轴旋转映射

用户要求将截面旋转轴从 x 轴改为 y 轴正方向逆时针旋转。当前代码中的截面旋转逻辑是绕 x 轴旋转（平面方程涉及 y 和 z 的变换）。需要改为绕 y 轴正方向逆时针旋转。在数学坐标系中，绕 y 轴正方向逆时针旋转 θ 角，变换关系为：

- x' = x cosθ + z sinθ
- z' = -x sinθ + z cosθ

截面平面在旋转后的坐标系中是 z' = zVal（常数），即：
-x sinθ + z cosθ = zVal代入曲面方程 z = ax³ + bx - y²：
-x sinθ + (ax³ + bx - y²) cosθ = zVal
=> ax³ cosθ + bx cosθ - x sinθ - y² cosθ = zVal
=> y² cosθ = ax³ cosθ + bx cosθ - x sinθ - zVal当 cosθ ≠ 0 时：
y² = ax³ + bx - x tanθ - zVal/cosθ当 cosθ = 0（θ = 90°）时：
-x sinθ = zVal
=> x = -zVal/sinθ = -zVal（因为 sin90° = 1）
这是一个垂直于 x 轴的平面，x 固定。在 Three.js 中，y 轴是垂直向上的。绕 y 轴正方向逆时针旋转，在 Three.js 的右手坐标系中，rotation.y = θ 表示绕 y 轴逆时针旋转。对于平面 mesh，初始状态是水平面（rotation.x = -π/2，法向量 (0,1,0) 对应数学 z 轴正方向）。
绕 y 轴旋转 θ 后，法向量变为 (sinθ, 0, cosθ)。平面方程：sinθ * x + cosθ * z = zVal（在数学坐标中，z 是垂直方向）等等，让我重新理清坐标系。Three.js 坐标系：

- x: 水平右
- y: 垂直上
- z: 深度前

数学坐标系（曲面方程 z = ax³ + bx - y² 中）：

- x: 水平
- y: 水平（与 x 垂直）
- z: 垂直

在 Three.js 中，我们把数学 x 映射到 Three.js x，数学 y 映射到 Three.js z，数学 z 映射到 Three.js y。所以曲面在 Three.js 中是：y_three = a*x_three³ + b*x_three - z_three²截面平面绕 y 轴（Three.js 的 y 轴，即数学的 z 轴）旋转... 不对。用户说"绕 y 轴正方向逆时针旋转"。这里的 y 轴应该是指数学坐标系的 y 轴（即 Three.js 的 z 轴），因为曲面的 y 是水平方向。但用户可能指的是 Three.js 的 y 轴。让我重新理解。在数学表达式 z = ax³ + bx - y² 中：

- x 和 y 是水平面内的坐标
- z 是垂直高度

如果截面绕 y 轴（数学 y 轴，即水平方向）旋转，这意味着截面平面在 x-z 平面内旋转。在 Three.js 中，数学 y 对应 Three.js z。所以绕数学 y 轴旋转 = 绕 Three.js z 轴旋转。但用户也可能指的是 Three.js 的 y 轴（垂直轴）。这会导致截面在 x-y 平面内旋转，这与"截面"的通常理解不太一致。让我重新看用户的原始需求。用户说"控制截面与x轴的夹角θ"，之前我理解为绕 x 轴旋转。现在改为"绕 y 轴正方向逆时针旋转"。如果截面与 x 轴的夹角是 θ，绕 y 轴旋转... 这有点矛盾。让我重新理解：用户可能想要的是截面绕垂直轴（y 轴）旋转，这样截面从水平逐渐变为竖直，但旋转轴是垂直的。等等，让我重新思考。在三维空间中：

- 初始截面是水平面（z = const）
- 绕 y 轴（垂直轴）旋转 θ 角后，截面变成倾斜面
- 在数学坐标中，绕 y 轴（水平轴，因为数学 y 是水平方向）旋转...

我觉得用户的意思是在数学坐标系中，绕 y 轴（水平方向，与 x 垂直）旋转。这对应于 Three.js 中绕 z 轴旋转。但用户明确说"绕 y 轴正方向逆时针旋转"。在 Three.js 中 y 轴是垂直向上的。如果绕垂直轴旋转，截面平面会从水平面逐渐倾斜，像一扇门绕垂直轴打开。让我这样理解：用户想要的是截面平面绕垂直轴（Three.js y 轴）逆时针旋转。在 Three.js 中：

- 初始平面是水平的（法向量 (0,1,0)）
- 绕 y 轴旋转 θ 后，法向量变为 (sinθ, 0, cosθ) 如果旋转是绕 y 轴的话...

不对。绕 y 轴旋转，y 分量不变。初始法向量 (0,1,0) 绕 y 轴旋转后仍然是 (0,1,0)。这意味着平面仍然是水平的！所以如果绕 Three.js 的 y 轴（垂直轴）旋转，水平面旋转后仍然是水平面。这不对。那用户说的 y 轴应该是数学坐标系的 y 轴。在数学坐标系中，y 是水平方向（在 x-y 平面内）。绕数学 y 轴旋转意味着在 x-z 平面内旋转截面。在 Three.js 中，数学 y 对应 Three.js z。所以绕数学 y 轴旋转 = 绕 Three.js z 轴旋转。绕 Three.js z 轴旋转 θ 角：

- 初始平面法向量 (0,1,0)
- 绕 z 轴旋转 θ 后，法向量变为 (-sinθ, cosθ, 0) 如果按右手定则...

等等，让我用旋转矩阵。绕 z 轴旋转 θ：
x' = x cosθ - y sinθ
y' = x sinθ + y cosθ
z' = z法向量 (0,1,0) 旋转后：
x' = 0*cosθ - 1*sinθ = -sinθ
y' = 0*sinθ + 1*cosθ = cosθ
z' = 0所以法向量是 (-sinθ, cosθ, 0)。平面方程：-sinθ * x + cosθ * y = zVal在数学坐标中（x_math = x_three, y_math = z_three, z_math = y_three）：
-sinθ * x_math + cosθ * z_math = zVal但 z_math = a*x_math³ + b*x_math - y_math²，所以：
-sinθ * x_math + cosθ * (a*x_math³ + b*x_math - y_math²) = zVal
cosθ * (a*x_math³ + b*x_math - y_math²) - sinθ * x_math = zVal
y_math² * cosθ = a*x_math³*cosθ + b*x_math*cosθ - sinθ*x_math - zVal当 cosθ ≠ 0：
y² = ax³ + bx - x*tanθ - zVal/cosθ这和之前绕 x 轴旋转的公式形式一样！但几何意义不同：

- 之前是绕 x 轴旋转（在 y-z 平面内旋转）
- 现在是绕 y 轴旋转（在 x-z 平面内旋转）

但用户之前说"截面与x轴的夹角θ"，如果绕 y 轴旋转，截面与 x 轴的夹角确实会变化。让我再仔细想。用户说"改为控制截面绕y轴正方向逆时针旋转"。在 Three.js 中，如果用户指的是 Three.js 的 y 轴（垂直轴），那水平面绕垂直轴旋转仍然是水平面，没有意义。所以用户一定指的是数学坐标系的 y 轴。在数学坐标系中，y 轴是水平的（在 x-y 平面内，与 x 垂直）。绕 y 轴旋转就是在 x-z 平面内旋转。在 Three.js 中，数学 y 对应 Three.js z。所以绕数学 y 轴旋转 = 绕 Three.js z 轴旋转。让我修改代码：

1. 平面在 Three.js 中绕 z 轴旋转
2. 数学截面方程：-sinθ * x + cosθ * z = zVal
3. 2D 绘图和 3D 截面线都需要相应修改

等等，让我再验证一下。用户说"绕 y 轴正方向逆时针旋转"。在右手坐标系中，y 轴正方向朝上。绕 y 轴逆时针旋转，从 y 轴正方向向下看是逆时针。在数学坐标系 (x, y, z) 中，z 朝上。从 y 轴正方向看，x-z 平面。逆时针旋转意味着 x 转向 z。旋转矩阵（绕 y 轴旋转 θ）：
x' = x cosθ + z sinθ
y' = y
z' = -x sinθ + z cosθ截面平面在旋转后的坐标系中是 z' = zVal：
-x sinθ + z cosθ = zVal代入 z = ax³ + bx - y²：
-x sinθ + (ax³ + bx - y²) cosθ = zVal
ax³ cosθ + bx cosθ - y² cosθ - x sinθ = zVal
y² cosθ = ax³ cosθ + bx cosθ - x sinθ - zVal当 cosθ ≠ 0：
y² = ax³ + bx - x tanθ - zVal/cosθ当 cosθ = 0（θ = 90°）：
-x sinθ = zVal
x = -zVal/sinθ = -zVal（因为 sin90°=1）这是一个垂直于 x 轴的平面（x = -zVal）。在 Three.js 中：

- 数学 x → Three.js x
- 数学 y → Three.js z
- 数学 z → Three.js y

所以绕数学 y 轴旋转 = 绕 Three.js z 轴旋转。在 Three.js 中，平面 mesh 初始是水平的（rotation.x = -π/2，法向量 (0,1,0) 即数学 z 方向）。要让它绕 Three.js z 轴旋转，我们需要：
rotation.z = θ这样法向量 (0,1,0) 绕 z 轴旋转 θ 后变为 (-sinθ, cosθ, 0)。平面方程在 Three.js 坐标中：
-sinθ * x_three + cosθ * y_three = zVal但 y_three = z_math，所以：
-sinθ * x_math + cosθ * z_math = zVal这和上面的数学推导一致。平面位置：需要让平面经过某个满足方程的点。例如当 x=0 时，cosθ * y = zVal，所以 y = zVal/cosθ（当 cosθ≠0）。
在 Three.js 中，平面 mesh 的中心应该在 (0, zVal/cosθ, 0)（当 cosθ≠0）。
当 cosθ=0 时，-sinθ * x = zVal，x = -zVal/sinθ = -zVal，平面中心在 (-zVal, 0, 0)。让我修改代码。需要修改的部分：

1. getIntervals 函数中的数学公式
2. drawSection2D 中的 y 计算
3. updateSection3D 中的平面旋转和位置
4. 3D 截面线的点计算

让我重新整理所有需要修改的代码块。首先，getIntervals：
D(x) = sin²θ + 4*cosθ*(ax³cosθ + bx cosθ - x sinθ - zVal) 当 cosθ≠0
实际上从 y² = ax³ + bx - x tanθ - zVal/cosθ 来看：
f(x) = ax³ + bx - x tanθ - zVal/cosθ
D(x) = f(x)（因为 y² = f(x)，需要 f(x) ≥ 0）当 cosθ = 0 时，x = -zVal，是单个点。让我重新用判别式法来统一处理。从方程：cosθ * y² + sinθ * x - cosθ * (ax³ + bx) + zVal = 0
不对，让我重新推导。原始方程：-x sinθ + z cosθ = zVal，其中 z = ax³ + bx - y²
=> -x sinθ + (ax³ + bx - y²) cosθ = zVal
=> ax³ cosθ + bx cosθ - x sinθ - y² cosθ = zVal
=> y² cosθ = ax³ cosθ + bx cosθ - x sinθ - zVal当 cosθ ≠ 0：
y² = ax³ + bx - x tanθ - zVal/cosθ当 cosθ = 0：
-x sinθ = zVal
=> x = -zVal/sinθ对于 getIntervals，当 cosθ ≠ 0 时，找 f(x) = ax³ + bx - x tanθ - zVal/cosθ ≥ 0 的区间。
当 cosθ = 0 时，只有一个 x 值。对于 2D 绘图，在区间内对每个 x 计算 y = ±√f(x)。对于 3D，截面线是在 Three.js 中绕 z 轴旋转的平面与曲面的交线。让我直接修改代码中的相关部分。由于工具步数限制，我尽量高效地完成。实际上，我发现之前绕 x 轴旋转的公式和绕 y 轴旋转的公式在形式上是相同的：

- 绕 x 轴：y² = ax³ + bx - zVal/cosθ + ...（涉及 y 和 z 的变换）
- 绕 y 轴：y² = ax³ + bx - x tanθ - zVal/cosθ

等等，让我重新检查绕 x 轴的情况。之前绕 x 轴旋转的推导：
平面方程：-sinθ * y + cosθ * z = zVal
代入 z = ax³ + bx - y²：
-sinθ * y + cosθ * (ax³ + bx - y²) = zVal
cosθ * (ax³ + bx) - sinθ * y - cosθ * y² = zVal
cosθ * y² + sinθ * y + zVal - cosθ * (ax³ + bx) = 0这是关于 y 的二次方程！不是简单的 y² = ...我之前写错了！让我重新推导绕 x 轴的情况。对于绕 x 轴旋转 θ：
在数学坐标中，绕 x 轴旋转：
y' = y cosθ + z sinθ
z' = -y sinθ + z cosθ截面平面 z' = zVal：
-y sinθ + z cosθ = zVal
z cosθ = y sinθ + zVal
z = y tanθ + zVal/cosθ（当 cosθ ≠ 0）代入 z = ax³ + bx - y²：
ax³ + bx - y² = y tanθ + zVal/cosθ
y² + y tanθ + zVal/cosθ - ax³ - bx = 0这是关于 y 的二次方程：
y = [-tanθ ± √(tan²θ - 4*(zVal/cosθ - ax³ - bx))] / 2判别式：D = tan²θ - 4*(zVal/cosθ - ax³ - bx) = tan²θ + 4*ax³ + 4*bx - 4*zVal/cosθ当 cosθ = 0（θ = 90°）：
-y sinθ = zVal
y = -zVal/sinθ = -zVal
这是一个垂直于 y 轴的平面。好的，所以绕 x 轴旋转时，y 满足二次方程，不是简单的 y² = ...而绕 y 轴旋转时：
-x sinθ + z cosθ = zVal
z = x tanθ + zVal/cosθ（当 cosθ ≠ 0）代入 z = ax³ + bx - y²：
ax³ + bx - y² = x tanθ + zVal/cosθ
y² = ax³ + bx - x tanθ - zVal/cosθ这是简单的 y² = f(x) 形式！和原来的水平截面形式一样，只是 f(x) 变了。当 cosθ = 0（θ = 90°）：
-x sinθ = zVal
x = -zVal
垂直于 x 轴的平面。所以绕 y 轴旋转的数学处理更简单！让我修改代码。在 Three.js 中：

- 绕数学 y 轴旋转 = 绕 Three.js z 轴旋转
- 平面 mesh 的 rotation.z = θ
- 平面位置：当 cosθ ≠ 0 时，平面经过 (0, zVal/cosθ, 0)，即 position.set(0, zVal/cosθ, 0)
- 当 cosθ = 0 时，平面经过 (-zVal, 0, 0)，即 position.set(-zVal, 0, 0)

3D 截面线：
对于每个 x，y² = ax³ + bx - x tanθ - zVal/cosθ
y = ±√(...)
z = ax³ + bx - y²在 Three.js 坐标中：
x_three = x
y_three = z = ax³ + bx - y²
z_three = y让我修改代码。由于步数限制，我直接输出完整的修改后 HTML。

已改为绕 **y 轴正方向逆时针旋转**。核心变化：截面在 **x-z 平面**内旋转（数学 y 轴即水平方向），数学上截面方程变为 `-x·sinθ + z·cosθ = zVal`，代入曲面后得到 `y² = ax³ + bx − x·tanθ − zVal/cosθ`，形式简洁且与水平截面兼容。Three.js 中对应绕 **z 轴**旋转平面。

HTML 预览 复制

```html
<!DOCTYPE html>
<html lang="en">
<head>
<meta charset="UTF-8">
<meta name="viewport" content="width=device-width, initial-scale=1.0">
<title>z = ax³ + bx - y² — Section Curve Demo</title>
<style>
* { margin: 0; padding: 0; box-sizing: border-box; }
body {
  width: 100vw; height: 100vh;
  background: #0b0f19; color: #e0e6f0;
  font-family: "Segoe UI", "PingFang SC", "Microsoft YaHei", sans-serif;
  overflow: hidden;
}
#app {
  width: 100%; height: 100%;
  display: flex; flex-direction: column;
}
.toolbar {
  display: flex;
  flex-direction: column;
  gap: 6px;
  padding: 8px 14px;
  background: #111827;
  border-bottom: 1px solid #1f2937;
  z-index: 10;
}
.toolbar-row {
  display: flex;
  align-items: center;
  gap: 10px;
  width: 100%;
}
.toolbar .title {
  font-weight: 700; font-size: 14px; color: #60a5fa; white-space: nowrap;
}
.radio-group {
  display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 4px;
}
.radio-label {
  padding: 4px 12px;
  border-radius: 5px;
  background: #1f2937;
  color: #9ca3af;
  font-size: 13px;
  cursor: pointer;
  border: 1px solid #374151;
  font-weight: 600;
  transition: all 0.15s;
}
.radio-label.active {
  background: #2563eb;
  color: #fff;
  border-color: #2563eb;
}
.radio-label input { position: absolute; opacity: 0; width: 0; height: 0; pointer-events: none; }
.param-val {
  font-size: 14px;
  color: #60a5fa;
  font-weight: 700;
  font-family: monospace;
  min-width: 60px;
  text-align: right;
}
.toolbar input[type=range] {
  flex: 1;
  width: 100%;
  accent-color: #60a5fa;
}
.toolbar .btn {
  padding: 5px 12px; border-radius: 5px;
  border: none; font-size: 12px;
  cursor: pointer; font-weight: 600;
  white-space: nowrap;
}
.btn-primary { background: #2563eb; color: #fff; }
.btn-secondary { background: #1f2937; color: #9ca3af; }

.main {
  flex: 1;
  display: flex; flex-direction: column;
  overflow: hidden;
}
.panel {
  flex: 1;
  display: flex; flex-direction: column;
  min-height: 80px;
  border-bottom: 2px solid #1f2937;
  transition: flex 0.35s cubic-bezier(.4,0,.2,1);
  position: relative;
}
.panel.collapsed {
  flex: 0 0 32px;
}
.panel-header {
  display: flex;
  align-items: center;
  justify-content: space-between;
  padding: 6px 14px;
  background: #0f172a;
  border-bottom: 1px solid #1f2937;
  cursor: pointer; user-select: none;
}
.panel-header .left {
  display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 8px;
}
.panel-header .icon {
  font-size: 11px; color: #60a5fa;
  transition: transform 0.2s;
  display: inline-block;
}
.panel.collapsed .icon {
  transform: rotate(-90deg);
}
.panel-header .name {
  font-size: 13px; font-weight: 600; color: #e0e6f0;
}
.panel-header .hint {
  font-size: 11px; color: #6b7280;
}
.panel-body {
  flex: 1;
  position: relative;
  overflow: hidden;
}
.panel.collapsed .panel-body {
  display: none;
}
#three-container {
  width: 100%; height: 100%;
}
#canvas-2d {
  width: 100%; height: 100%;
  display: block;
}
</style>
</head>
<body>
<div id="app">

<div class="toolbar">
  <div class="toolbar-row">
    <span class="title">z = ax³ + bx - y²</span>
    <div class="radio-group">
      <label class="radio-label" data-value="a">
        <input type="radio" name="param" value="a"> a
      </label>
      <label class="radio-label" data-value="b">
        <input type="radio" name="param" value="b"> b
      </label>
      <label class="radio-label active" data-value="z">
        <input type="radio" name="param" value="z" checked> z
      </label>
    </div>
    <span id="param-val" class="param-val">0.00</span>
    <div style="flex:1;"></div>
  </div>
  <div class="toolbar-row">
    <input id="slider" type="range" min="-20" max="20" step="0.001" value="0">
  </div>
  <div class="toolbar-row">
    <button id="btn-play" class="btn btn-primary">▶ Play</button>
    <button id="btn-dir" class="btn btn-secondary">↓ Down</button>
    <div style="flex:1;"></div>
    <div style="display:flex;align-items:center;gap:8px;margin-left:12px;">
      <span style="font-size:13px;color:#9ca3af;font-weight:600;">θ</span>
      <input id="theta-slider" type="range" min="0" max="180" step="0.1" value="0" style="width:140px;accent-color:#fbbf24;">
      <span id="theta-val" class="param-val" style="min-width:50px;color:#fbbf24;">0.0°</span>
    </div>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="main">
  <div class="panel" id="panel-3d">
    <div class="panel-header" id="header-3d">
      <div class="left">
        <span class="icon" id="icon-3d">▼</span>
        <span class="name">3D Surface & Section</span>
        <span class="hint" id="hint-3d">— z = -1.00x³ + 1.00x - y²</span>
      </div>
    </div>
    <div class="panel-body" id="body-3d">
      <div id="three-container"></div>
    </div>
  </div>
  <div class="panel collapsed" id="panel-2d">
    <div class="panel-header" id="header-2d">
      <div class="left">
        <span class="icon" id="icon-2d">▼</span>
        <span class="name">2D Section Curve</span>
        <span class="hint" id="formula-hint">— y² = -1.00x³ + 1.00x + 0.00</span>
      </div>
    </div>
    <div class="panel-body" id="body-2d">
      <canvas id="canvas-2d"></canvas>
    </div>
  </div>
</div>

</div>

<script type="importmap">
{
  "imports": {
    "three": "https://unpkg.com/three@0.160.0/build/three.module.js",
    "three/addons/": "https://unpkg.com/three@0.160.0/examples/jsm/"
  }
}
</script>

<script type="module">
import * as THREE from "three";
import { OrbitControls } from "three/addons/controls/OrbitControls.js";

let a = -1, b = 1, zVal = 0, theta = 0;
let activeParam = "z";
let isPlaying = false, playDir = 1;
let animId = null, lastTime = 0;

const slider = document.getElementById("slider");
const paramVal = document.getElementById("param-val");
const formulaHint = document.getElementById("formula-hint");
const btnPlay = document.getElementById("btn-play");
const btnDir = document.getElementById("btn-dir");
const thetaSlider = document.getElementById("theta-slider");
const thetaVal = document.getElementById("theta-val");

const canvas2d = document.getElementById("canvas-2d");
const ctx2d = canvas2d.getContext("2d");
const xMin = -3, xMax = 3;

function resize2D() {
  const rect = canvas2d.parentElement.getBoundingClientRect();
  const dpr = window.devicePixelRatio || 1;
  canvas2d.width = rect.width * dpr;
  canvas2d.height = rect.height * dpr;
  ctx2d.setTransform(dpr, 0, 0, dpr, 0, 0);
  draw2D();
}

function toScreen(x, y) {
  const w = canvas2d.width / (window.devicePixelRatio || 1);
  const h = canvas2d.height / (window.devicePixelRatio || 1);
  const pad = 45;
  const sx = pad + (x - xMin) / (xMax - xMin) * (w - 2 * pad);
  const yMax = (h - 2 * pad) / (w - 2 * pad) * (xMax - xMin) / 2;
  const yMin = -yMax;
  const sy = h - pad - (y - yMin) / (yMax - yMin) * (h - 2 * pad);
  return [sx, sy];
}

function drawGrid2D() {
  const w = canvas2d.width / (window.devicePixelRatio || 1);
  const h = canvas2d.height / (window.devicePixelRatio || 1);
  const pad = 45;
  ctx2d.clearRect(0, 0, w, h);
  ctx2d.fillStyle = "#0b0f19";
  ctx2d.fillRect(0, 0, w, h);
  ctx2d.strokeStyle = "#1f2937";
  ctx2d.lineWidth = 1;
  for (let x = Math.ceil(xMin); x <= Math.floor(xMax); x++) {
    const [sx1, sy1] = toScreen(x, -100);
    const [sx2, sy2] = toScreen(x, 100);
    ctx2d.beginPath(); ctx2d.moveTo(sx1, sy1); ctx2d.lineTo(sx2, sy2); ctx2d.stroke();
  }
  const yMax = (h - 2 * pad) / (w - 2 * pad) * (xMax - xMin) / 2;
  for (let y = Math.ceil(-yMax); y <= Math.floor(yMax); y++) {
    const [sx1, sy1] = toScreen(-100, y);
    const [sx2, sy2] = toScreen(100, y);
    ctx2d.beginPath(); ctx2d.moveTo(sx1, sy1); ctx2d.lineTo(sx2, sy2); ctx2d.stroke();
  }
  ctx2d.strokeStyle = "#4b5563";
  ctx2d.lineWidth = 1.5;
  const [ox1, oy1] = toScreen(xMin, 0), [ox2, oy2] = toScreen(xMax, 0);
  ctx2d.beginPath(); ctx2d.moveTo(ox1, oy1); ctx2d.lineTo(ox2, oy2); ctx2d.stroke();
  const [oyx1, oyy1] = toScreen(0, -yMax), [oyx2, oyy2] = toScreen(0, yMax);
  ctx2d.beginPath(); ctx2d.moveTo(oyx1, oyy1); ctx2d.lineTo(oyx2, oyy2); ctx2d.stroke();
  ctx2d.fillStyle = "#6b7280";
  ctx2d.font = "11px sans-serif";
  ctx2d.textAlign = "center";
  for (let x = Math.ceil(xMin); x <= Math.floor(xMax); x++) {
    if (x === 0) continue;
    const [sx, sy] = toScreen(x, 0);
    ctx2d.fillText(x.toString(), sx, sy + 16);
  }
  ctx2d.textAlign = "right";
  for (let y = Math.ceil(-yMax); y <= Math.floor(yMax); y++) {
    if (y === 0) continue;
    const [sx, sy] = toScreen(0, y);
    ctx2d.fillText(y.toString(), sx - 5, sy + 3);
  }
  ctx2d.fillText("O", toScreen(0,0)[0]-5, toScreen(0,0)[1]+16);
}

// Section plane: -x·sinθ + z·cosθ = zVal  (rotated around y-axis, CCW)
// Substituting z = ax³ + bx - y²:
//   y²·cosθ = ax³·cosθ + bx·cosθ - x·sinθ - zVal
// When cosθ ≠ 0:  y² = ax³ + bx - x·tanθ - zVal/cosθ
// When cosθ = 0:  x = -zVal/sinθ  (vertical plane)
function getIntervals(aa, bb, zz, thDeg) {
  const th = thDeg * Math.PI / 180;
  const cosT = Math.cos(th);
  const sinT = Math.sin(th);
  const steps = 4000;
  const dxx = (xMax - xMin) / steps;

  function f(x) {
    if (Math.abs(cosT) < 1e-6) return -1;
    return aa*x*x*x + bb*x - x*Math.tan(th) - zz/cosT;
  }

  if (Math.abs(cosT) < 1e-6) {
    const xFixed = -zz / sinT;
    if (xFixed >= xMin && xFixed <= xMax) return [{ left: xFixed, right: xFixed, vertical: true }];
    return [];
  }

  const roots = [];
  for (let i = 0; i <= steps; i++) {
    const x = xMin + i * dxx;
    const fx = f(x);
    if (Math.abs(fx) < 1e-10) roots.push(x);
  }
  let prev = f(xMin);
  for (let i = 1; i <= steps; i++) {
    const x = xMin + i * dxx;
    const curr = f(x);
    if (prev * curr < 0) {
      let lo = x - dxx, hi = x;
      for (let k = 0; k < 25; k++) {
        const mid = (lo + hi) / 2;
        const vm = f(mid);
        if (Math.abs(vm) < 1e-12) { lo = hi = mid; break; }
        if (prev * vm < 0) hi = mid; else lo = mid;
      }
      roots.push((lo + hi) / 2);
    }
    prev = curr;
  }
  const uniq = [];
  for (const r of roots) {
    if (!uniq.some(u => Math.abs(u - r) < 0.001)) uniq.push(r);
  }
  uniq.sort((p, q) => p - q);
  const pts = [xMin, ...uniq, xMax];
  const intervals = [];
  for (let i = 0; i < pts.length - 1; i++) {
    const mid = (pts[i] + pts[i + 1]) / 2;
    if (f(mid) > 0) intervals.push({ left: pts[i], right: pts[i + 1] });
  }
  return intervals;
}

function drawSection2D() {
  const th = theta * Math.PI / 180;
  const cosT = Math.cos(th);
  const intervals = getIntervals(a, b, zVal, theta);
  ctx2d.strokeStyle = "#60a5fa";
  ctx2d.lineWidth = 2.5;

  if (intervals.length === 1 && intervals[0].vertical) {
    const xFixed = intervals[0].left;
    const [sx, syTop] = toScreen(xFixed, 5);
    const [sx2, syBot] = toScreen(xFixed, -5);
    ctx2d.setLineDash([5, 5]);
    ctx2d.beginPath(); ctx2d.moveTo(sx, syTop); ctx2d.lineTo(sx2, syBot); ctx2d.stroke();
    ctx2d.setLineDash([]);
    ctx2d.fillStyle = "#fbbf24"; ctx2d.font = "12px sans-serif"; ctx2d.textAlign = "center";
    ctx2d.fillText(`x = ${xFixed.toFixed(2)}`, sx, syTop - 8);
    return;
  }

  intervals.forEach(({ left, right }) => {
    const segSteps = Math.max(2, Math.ceil((right - left) / (xMax - xMin) * 800));
    const ddx = (right - left) / segSteps;
    const upper = [], lower = [];
    for (let i = 0; i <= segSteps; i++) {
      const x = left + i * ddx;
      const fx = a*x*x*x + b*x - x*Math.tan(th) - zVal/cosT;
      const y = Math.sqrt(Math.max(0, fx));
      upper.push(toScreen(x, y));
      lower.push(toScreen(x, -y));
    }
    const leftIsRoot = Math.abs(left - xMin) > 0.001;
    const rightIsRoot = Math.abs(right - xMax) > 0.001;
    const isClosed = leftIsRoot && rightIsRoot;
    if (isClosed) {
      const pts = [...upper, ...lower.reverse()];
      if (pts.length > 2) {
        ctx2d.beginPath(); ctx2d.moveTo(pts[0][0], pts[0][1]);
        for (let i = 1; i < pts.length; i++) ctx2d.lineTo(pts[i][0], pts[i][1]);
        ctx2d.stroke();
      }
    } else {
      if (upper.length > 1) {
        ctx2d.beginPath(); ctx2d.moveTo(upper[0][0], upper[0][1]);
        for (let i = 1; i < upper.length; i++) ctx2d.lineTo(upper[i][0], upper[i][1]);
        ctx2d.stroke();
      }
      if (lower.length > 1) {
        ctx2d.beginPath(); ctx2d.moveTo(lower[0][0], lower[0][1]);
        for (let i = 1; i < lower.length; i++) ctx2d.lineTo(lower[i][0], lower[i][1]);
        ctx2d.stroke();
      }
    }
  });

  ctx2d.fillStyle = "#fbbf24";
  const roots = [];
  intervals.forEach(({ left, right }) => {
    if (Math.abs(left - xMin) > 0.001) roots.push(left);
    if (Math.abs(right - xMax) > 0.001) roots.push(right);
  });
  const uniqRoots = [];
  for (const r of roots) {
    if (!uniqRoots.some(u => Math.abs(u - r) < 0.001)) uniqRoots.push(r);
  }
  for (const r of uniqRoots) {
    if (r >= xMin && r <= xMax) {
      const [sx, sy] = toScreen(r, 0);
      ctx2d.beginPath(); ctx2d.arc(sx, sy, 4, 0, Math.PI * 2); ctx2d.fill();
    }
  }
}

function draw2D() {
  drawGrid2D();
  drawSection2D();
}

const container3d = document.getElementById("three-container");
const scene = new THREE.Scene();
scene.background = new THREE.Color(0x0b0f19);
const camera = new THREE.PerspectiveCamera(45, 1, 0.1, 100);
camera.position.set(7, 5, 7);
const renderer = new THREE.WebGLRenderer({ antialias: true });
renderer.setPixelRatio(window.devicePixelRatio || 1);
container3d.appendChild(renderer.domElement);
const controls = new OrbitControls(camera, renderer.domElement);
controls.enableDamping = true; controls.dampingFactor = 0.08;

scene.add(new THREE.AmbientLight(0xffffff, 0.4));
const dirLight = new THREE.DirectionalLight(0xffffff, 0.8);
dirLight.position.set(5, 8, 5); scene.add(dirLight);
const dirLight2 = new THREE.DirectionalLight(0x60a5fa, 0.3);
dirLight2.position.set(-5, 2, -5); scene.add(dirLight2);

const axesHelper = new THREE.AxesHelper(1.33);
scene.add(axesHelper);

const xyPlaneGeo = new THREE.PlaneGeometry(20, 20);
const xyPlaneMat = new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({ color: 0x1f2937, side: THREE.DoubleSide, transparent: true, opacity: 0.25 });
const xyPlane = new THREE.Mesh(xyPlaneGeo, xyPlaneMat);
xyPlane.rotation.x = -Math.PI / 2; xyPlane.position.y = 0;
scene.add(xyPlane);

const gridSize = 20, gridDivs = 20;
const gridGeo = new THREE.BufferGeometry();
const gridPts = [];
for (let i = -gridDivs/2; i <= gridDivs/2; i++) {
  const v = i / gridDivs * gridSize;
  gridPts.push(new THREE.Vector3(-gridSize/2, 0, v));
  gridPts.push(new THREE.Vector3(gridSize/2, 0, v));
  gridPts.push(new THREE.Vector3(v, 0, -gridSize/2));
  gridPts.push(new THREE.Vector3(v, 0, gridSize/2));
}
gridGeo.setFromPoints(gridPts);
const gridMat = new THREE.LineBasicMaterial({ color: 0x374151, transparent: true, opacity: 0.25 });
const customGrid = new THREE.LineSegments(gridGeo, gridMat);
scene.add(customGrid);

const segX = 150, segY = 150;
const surfGeo = new THREE.PlaneGeometry(6, 10, segX, segY);
const surfOrig = [];
const pos0 = surfGeo.attributes.position;
for (let i = 0; i < pos0.count; i++) surfOrig.push({ x: pos0.getX(i), y: pos0.getY(i) });

const surfMat = new THREE.MeshPhongMaterial({ color: 0x1e3a5f, side: THREE.DoubleSide, transparent: true, opacity: 0.65, shininess: 60, flatShading: false });
const surfMesh = new THREE.Mesh(surfGeo, surfMat);
scene.add(surfMesh);

const wireGeo = surfGeo.clone();
const wireOrig = [];
const wpos0 = wireGeo.attributes.position;
for (let i = 0; i < wpos0.count; i++) wireOrig.push({ x: wpos0.getX(i), y: wpos0.getY(i) });
const wireMat = new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({ color: 0x60a5fa, wireframe: true, transparent: true, opacity: 0.15 });
const wireMesh = new THREE.Mesh(wireGeo, wireMat);
scene.add(wireMesh);

// Cutting plane: rotates around y-axis (Three.js z-axis, since math y -> Three.js z)
const planeGeo = new THREE.PlaneGeometry(20, 20);
const planeMat = new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({ color: 0xfbbf24, side: THREE.DoubleSide, transparent: true, opacity: 0.12 });
const cutPlane = new THREE.Mesh(planeGeo, planeMat);
cutPlane.rotation.x = -Math.PI / 2;
scene.add(cutPlane);

const sectionMat = new THREE.LineBasicMaterial({ color: 0xfbbf24, linewidth: 3 });
let sectionLines = [];

function updateSurface() {
  const pos = surfGeo.attributes.position;
  for (let i = 0; i < pos.count; i++) {
    const tx = surfOrig[i].x, ty = surfOrig[i].y;
    const math_z = a*tx*tx*tx + b*tx - ty*ty;
    pos.setXYZ(i, tx, math_z, ty);
  }
  pos.needsUpdate = true; surfGeo.computeVertexNormals();
  const wpos = wireMesh.geometry.attributes.position;
  for (let i = 0; i < wpos.count; i++) {
    const tx = wireOrig[i].x, ty = wireOrig[i].y;
    const math_z = a*tx*tx*tx + b*tx - ty*ty;
    wpos.setXYZ(i, tx, math_z, ty);
  }
  wpos.needsUpdate = true; wireMesh.geometry.computeVertexNormals();
}

function updateSection3D() {
  const th = theta * Math.PI / 180;
  const cosT = Math.cos(th);
  const sinT = Math.sin(th);

  // Math y-axis corresponds to Three.js z-axis.
  // Rotate around Three.js z-axis by theta.
  cutPlane.rotation.set(-Math.PI/2, 0, 0);
  cutPlane.rotation.z = th;

  // Plane equation in Three.js: -sinθ·x + cosθ·y = zVal
  // Position: when x=0, y = zVal/cosθ (math z = zVal/cosθ)
  if (Math.abs(cosT) > 1e-6) {
    cutPlane.position.set(0, zVal / cosT, 0);
  } else {
    cutPlane.position.set(-zVal, 0, 0);
  }

  sectionLines.forEach(l => scene.remove(l));
  sectionLines = [];
  const intervals = getIntervals(a, b, zVal, theta);

  if (intervals.length === 1 && intervals[0].vertical) {
    const xFixed = intervals[0].left;
    const pts = [];
    const yMax = 5, segSteps = 200;
    for (let i = 0; i <= segSteps; i++) {
      const y = -yMax + (2*yMax*i)/segSteps;
      const z = a*xFixed*xFixed*xFixed + b*xFixed - y*y;
      pts.push(new THREE.Vector3(xFixed, z, y));
    }
    const geo = new THREE.BufferGeometry().setFromPoints(pts);
    const line = new THREE.Line(geo, sectionMat);
    scene.add(line); sectionLines.push(line);
    return;
  }

  intervals.forEach(({ left, right }) => {
    const segSteps = Math.max(2, Math.ceil((right - left) / (xMax - xMin) * 600));
    const ddx = (right - left) / segSteps;
    const ptsUpper = [], ptsLower = [];
    for (let i = 0; i <= segSteps; i++) {
      const x = left + i * ddx;
      const fx = a*x*x*x + b*x - x*Math.tan(th) - zVal/cosT;
      const y = Math.sqrt(Math.max(0, fx));
      const z = a*x*x*x + b*x - y*y;
      ptsUpper.push(new THREE.Vector3(x, z, y));
      ptsLower.push(new THREE.Vector3(x, z, -y));
    }
    const leftIsRoot = Math.abs(left - xMin) > 0.001;
    const rightIsRoot = Math.abs(right - xMax) > 0.001;
    const isClosed = leftIsRoot && rightIsRoot;
    if (isClosed) {
      const pts = [...ptsUpper, ...ptsLower.reverse()];
      if (pts.length > 2) {
        const geo = new THREE.BufferGeometry().setFromPoints(pts);
        const line = new THREE.Line(geo, sectionMat);
        scene.add(line); sectionLines.push(line);
      }
    } else {
      if (ptsUpper.length > 1) {
        const geo = new THREE.BufferGeometry().setFromPoints(ptsUpper);
        const line = new THREE.Line(geo, sectionMat);
        scene.add(line); sectionLines.push(line);
      }
      if (ptsLower.length > 1) {
        const geo = new THREE.BufferGeometry().setFromPoints(ptsLower);
        const line = new THREE.Line(geo, sectionMat);
        scene.add(line); sectionLines.push(line);
      }
    }
  });
}

function resize3D() {
  const rect = container3d.getBoundingClientRect();
  camera.aspect = rect.width / rect.height;
  camera.updateProjectionMatrix();
  renderer.setSize(rect.width, rect.height);
}

function animate3D() {
  requestAnimationFrame(animate3D);
  controls.update();
  renderer.render(scene, camera);
}

function setupPanel(headerId, panelId) {
  const header = document.getElementById(headerId);
  const panel = document.getElementById(panelId);
  header.addEventListener("click", () => {
    panel.classList.toggle("collapsed");
    setTimeout(() => { resize2D(); resize3D(); }, 350);
  });
}
setupPanel("header-3d", "panel-3d");
setupPanel("header-2d", "panel-2d");

function updateFormula() {
  const sa = a.toFixed(2);
  const sb = b >= 0 ? "+ " + b.toFixed(2) : "- " + Math.abs(b).toFixed(2);
  const sz = zVal >= 0 ? "+ " + zVal.toFixed(2) : "- " + Math.abs(zVal).toFixed(2);
  formulaHint.textContent = `— y² = ${sa}x³ ${sb}x ${sz}`;
  const hint3d = document.getElementById("hint-3d");
  if (hint3d) {
    const sa3 = a.toFixed(2);
    const sb3 = b >= 0 ? "+ " + b.toFixed(2) : "- " + Math.abs(b).toFixed(2);
    hint3d.textContent = `— z = ${sa3}x³ ${sb3}x - y²`;
  }
}

function updateSliderConfig() {
  if (activeParam === "a") {
    slider.min = -10; slider.max = 20; slider.step = 0.01; slider.value = a;
    paramVal.textContent = a.toFixed(2);
  } else if (activeParam === "b") {
    slider.min = -10; slider.max = 10; slider.step = 0.1; slider.value = b;
    paramVal.textContent = b.toFixed(2);
  } else {
    slider.min = -20; slider.max = 20; slider.step = 0.001; slider.value = zVal;
    paramVal.textContent = zVal.toFixed(2);
  }
  updateFormula();
}

function updateZ(val) {
  zVal = Math.max(-20, Math.min(20, parseFloat(val) || 0));
  draw2D(); updateSection3D(); updateFormula();
  if (activeParam === "z") updateSliderConfig();
}

function updateA() {
  a = Math.max(-10, Math.min(20, a));
  draw2D(); updateSurface(); updateSection3D();
  if (activeParam === "a") updateSliderConfig(); else updateFormula();
}

function updateB() {
  b = Math.max(-10, Math.min(10, b));
  draw2D(); updateSurface(); updateSection3D();
  if (activeParam === "b") updateSliderConfig(); else updateFormula();
}

slider.addEventListener("input", () => {
  const val = parseFloat(slider.value);
  if (activeParam === "a") { a = val; updateA(); }
  else if (activeParam === "b") { b = val; updateB(); }
  else { updateZ(val); }
  updateSliderConfig();
});

function setActiveParam(val) {
  activeParam = val;
  document.querySelectorAll(".radio-label").forEach(l => l.classList.remove("active"));
  document.querySelectorAll('input[name="param"]').forEach(r => {
    r.checked = (r.value === val);
    if (r.value === val) r.parentElement.classList.add("active");
  });
  updateSliderConfig();
}

document.querySelectorAll('input[name="param"]').forEach(radio => {
  radio.addEventListener("change", () => setActiveParam(radio.value));
});
document.querySelectorAll(".radio-label").forEach(label => {
  label.addEventListener("click", (e) => {
    if (e.target.tagName === "INPUT") return;
    setActiveParam(label.dataset.value);
  });
});

btnPlay.addEventListener("click", () => {
  if (isPlaying) {
    isPlaying = false; btnPlay.textContent = "▶ Play"; btnPlay.style.background = "#2563eb";
    cancelAnimationFrame(animId);
  } else {
    isPlaying = true; btnPlay.textContent = "⏸ Pause"; btnPlay.style.background = "#ea580c";
    lastTime = 0;
    if (zVal <= -20 && playDir === 1) { playDir = -1; btnDir.textContent = "↑ Up"; }
    if (zVal >= 20 && playDir === -1) { playDir = 1; btnDir.textContent = "↓ Down"; }
    animId = requestAnimationFrame(playLoop);
  }
});

function playLoop(timestamp) {
  if (!isPlaying) return;
  if (!lastTime) lastTime = timestamp;
  const dt = (timestamp - lastTime) / 1000;
  lastTime = timestamp;
  const speed = 2.5;
  let newZ = zVal - playDir * speed * dt;
  if (playDir === 1 && newZ <= -20) { newZ = -20; isPlaying = false; btnPlay.textContent = "▶ Play"; btnPlay.style.background = "#2563eb"; }
  if (playDir === -1 && newZ >= 20) { newZ = 20; isPlaying = false; btnPlay.textContent = "▶ Play"; btnPlay.style.background = "#2563eb"; }
  updateZ(newZ);
  if (isPlaying) animId = requestAnimationFrame(playLoop);
}

btnDir.addEventListener("click", () => {
  playDir = playDir === 1 ? -1 : 1;
  btnDir.textContent = playDir === 1 ? "↓ Down" : "↑ Up";
});

thetaSlider.addEventListener("input", () => {
  theta = parseFloat(thetaSlider.value);
  thetaVal.textContent = theta.toFixed(1) + "°";
  draw2D(); updateSection3D(); updateFormula();
});

setTimeout(() => {
  resize2D(); resize3D();
  updateSurface(); updateSection3D();
  animate3D(); updateSliderConfig(); updateFormula();
  thetaVal.textContent = theta.toFixed(1) + "°";
}, 100);

window.addEventListener("resize", () => { resize2D(); resize3D(); });
</script>
</body>
</html>
```

**关键修改说明：**

- **旋转轴**：截面绕数学 **y 轴**（水平方向，对应 Three.js 的 **z 轴**）正方向逆时针旋转。
- **平面方程**：`-x·sinθ + z·cosθ = zVal`
- **交线公式**：`y² = ax³ + bx − x·tanθ − zVal/cosθ`（`cosθ≠0` 时）；`θ=90°` 时退化为垂直平面 `x = −zVal`
- **3D 平面**：`cutPlane.rotation.z = θ`，位置随 `zVal/cosθ` 或 `−zVal` 动态调整
- **2D/3D 同步**：θ 滑块拖动时，2D 曲线、3D 截面线、公式提示同时刷新
